n-left:0}.v32Actions .btn{flex:1 1 125px}}
`;document.head.appendChild(v32Style);
const v32MoveKey=m=>(m.name||'')+'|'+(m.type||'normal')+'|'+(m.kind||'bolt');
const v32BaseSkills=skillSet;
function v32Catalogue(monster){
 if(!monster||!D[monster.id])return [];
 const p=D[monster.id],base=v32BaseSkills(monster),known=new Map();
 function add(move,level=1,tm=false){if(!move||!move.name)return;const item={...move,cat:move.cat||(move.power?'special':'status')};if(item.kind==='homing')item.cd=Math.max(10000,item.cd||0);
  const key=v32MoveKey(item);if(!known.has(key))known.set(key,{key,move:item,level,tm,available:!tm?monster.level>=level:save.v32.tms.includes(item.type)});
 }
 for(const x of base)add(x);
 const types=[...new Set([p.type,p.type2].filter(Boolean))];
 for(const t of types){const e=V32_ADD_MOVES[t];if(e)add(e,e.level);}
 if(p.type!=='normal'&&p.type2!=='normal')add(V32_ADD_MOVES.normal,Math.max(10,V32_ADD_MOVES.normal.level));
 for(const [type,m] of Object.entries(V32_TM)){
  if(type==='normal'||types.includes(type))add({...m,cat:'special',name:m.name.replace(/^TM /,''),type},1,true);
 }
 return [...known.values()];
}
function v32DefaultSlots(m){const b=v32BaseSkills(m),pool=v32Catalogue(m);return b.map(move=>v32MoveKey(move)).map((key,i)=>pool.some(x=>x.key===key)?key:pool[i]?.key).slice(0,4);}
skillSet=function(m){
 const basic=v32BaseSkills(m);if(!m||!Array.isArray(m.v32Slots)||m.v32Slots.length!==4)return basic;
 const pool=v32Catalogue(m);const base=v32DefaultSlots(m);
 return m.v32Slots.map((key,i)=>{
  const match=pool.find(x=>x.key===key&&x.available);
  return match?{...match.move}:{...basic[i]};
 });
};
function v32MoveScreen(m){
 if(battle?.active)return;
 v32Ensure();const all=[...save.team,...save.box];if(!all.includes(m))return;
 const cat=v32Catalogue(m),selected=Array.isArray(m.v32Slots)?m.v32Slots:v32DefaultSlots(m);let slot=0;
 function paint(){
  const rows=cat.map(x=>{
    const selectedThis=selected[slot]===x.key;let unlock=x.tm?'TM da loja':`Nv. ${x.level}`;
    const locked=!x.available;
    return `<button type="button" class="v32MoveRow ${locked?'locked':''} ${selectedThis?'selected':''}" data-v32-learn="${cat.indexOf(x)}" ${locked?'disabled':''}><span><b>${x.move.icon||'✨'} ${x.move.name}</b><small>${TYPE_NAME[x.move.type]||x.move.type} · ${x.move.power||0} força · ${x.move.cost||0} energia · ${(x.move.cd/1000).toFixed(1)}s ${locked?'· Desbloqueio: '+unlock:''}</small></span><span class="pillSmall">${selectedThis?'EQUIPADO':locked?'BLOQUEADO':'APRENDER'}</span></button>`;
  }).join('');
  overlay(`<div class="sectionhead"><div><div class="overline">GERENCIAR GOLPES · V32</div><h2>🧠 ${D[m.id].name} · Nv. ${m.level}</h2></div><button class="btn tiny ghost" id="v32ReturnTeam">✕</button></div><p class="desc">Selecione uma das quatro posições e depois escolha um golpe disponível. A troca é permanente até você alterar novamente, e não custa moedas.</p><div class="v32Banner">${v32Nature(m).name}: ${v32Nature(m).desc}. Novos golpes aparecem ao subir de nível ou comprar TMs compatíveis no PokéMart.</div><div class="v32Grid"><div class="v32Box"><h3>Quatro golpes equipados</h3>${selected.map((k,i)=>{const x=cat.find(c=>c.key===k)||cat.find(c=>c.key===v32DefaultSlots(m)[i]);const p=x?.move||skillSet(m)[i];return `<button type="button" class="v32MoveRow ${slot===i?'selected':''}" data-v32-slot="${i}"><span><b>${i+1}. ${p?.icon||'✨'} ${p?.name||'Golpe'}</b><small>${TYPE_NAME[p?.type]||'Normal'} · ${p?.cost||0} energia</small></span><span class="pillSmall">${slot===i?'TROCAR':'ESCOLHER'}</span></button>`}).join('')}</div><div class="v32Box"><h3>Golpes disponíveis (${cat.filter(x=>x.available).length})</h3><div style="max-height:55vh;overflow-y:auto">${rows}</div></div></div><div class="v32Actions"><button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="v32ResetMoves">↻ Golpes padrão</button><button type="button" class="btn primary" id="v32DoneMoves">✓ Voltar à equipe</button></div>`);
  $('v32ReturnTeam').onclick=showTeam;$('v32DoneMoves').onclick=showTeam;
  $('v32ResetMoves').onclick=()=>{m.v32Slots=null;persist();showTeam()};
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-v32-slot]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{slot=Number(b.dataset.v32Slot);paint()});
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-v32-learn]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
   const e=cat[Number(b.dataset.v32Learn)];if(!e?.available)return;
   if(selected.some((k,i)=>k===e.key&&i!==slot))return v31Toast('Esse golpe já está em outro espaço.');
   selected[slot]=e.key;m.v32Slots=selected.slice();persist();v31Toast(e.move.name+' equipado!');paint();
  });
 }
 paint();
}

function v32Nature(m){return V32_NATURES.find(n=>n.id===m?.v32Nature)||V32_NATURES[3];}
const v32DamageOld=damage;
damage=function(att,def,type,power){const result=v32DamageOld(att,def,type,power);if(!result||result.hit<=0)return result;
 let mod=v32Nature(att).atk/v32Nature(def).def;
 const currentWeather=battle?.v32Weather;const w=V32_WEATHERS[currentWeather];
 if(w){mod*=w.bonus[type]||1;mod*=w.penalty[type]||1;}
 result.hit=Math.max(1,Math.round(result.hit*mod));return result;
};

const v32FinishOld=finishBattle;
finishBattle=function(win){
 if(!battle?.active||!['tower','league'].includes(battle.v32Mode))return v32FinishOld(win);
 const mode=battle.v32Mode,stage=mode==='tower'?battle.v32Floor:battle.v32LeagueStage;
 battle.active=false;stopTimers();battle=null;
 if(mode==='tower'){
  const info=save.v32.tower;
  if(win){info.best=Math.max(info.best,stage);info.wins=(info.wins||0)+1;info.floor=Math.min(999,stage+1);
   const money=20+Math.min(120,stage*4)+(stage%10===0?200:0);save.coins+=money;save.wins++;
   for(const m of save.team){if(m.hp>0){awardXP(m,24+stage);m.hp=Math.min(m.maxHp,m.hp+Math.ceil(m.maxHp*.17));}}
   if(stage%10===0)save.superPotions=(save.superPotions||0)+1;
   persist();resultScreen('♾️ ANDAR '+stage+' CONCLUÍDO!','+ moedas e experiência! Próximo andar: '+info.floor+(stage%10===0?' · Chefe derrotado! Superpoção recebida.':''),true,false);
  }else{info.floor=1;save.losses++;for(const m of save.team)m.hp=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(m.maxHp*.55));persist();resultScreen('♾️ TORRE · DESAFIO ENCERRADO','Seu recorde foi mantido. Reinicie no primeiro andar quando quiser.',false,false);}
 }else{
  const info=save.v32.league;if(win){save.wins++;save.coins+=120+(stage*50);for(const m of save.team){if(m.hp>0){awardXP(m,45+stage*10);m.hp=Math.min(m.maxHp,m.hp+Math.ceil(m.maxHp*.22));}}
   info.wins=(info.wins||0)+1;info.stage=(stage+1)%5;if(stage===4){info.championships=(info.championships||0)+1;save.coins+=400;}persist();
   resultScreen(stage===4?'🏆 CAMPEÃO NOVAMENTE!':'🏆 REVANCHE VENCIDA','Vitória contra '+['Lorelei','Bruno','Agatha','Lance','Blue'][stage]+'. '+(stage===4?'Torneio concluído! Bônus de 400 moedas.':'Próximo desafiante desbloqueado.'),true,false);
  }else{info.stage=0;save.losses++;for(const m of save.team)m.hp=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(m.maxHp*.5));persist();resultScreen('💔 REVANCHE ENCERRADA','A sequência da Liga volta para Lorelei; sua campanha original permanece salva.',false,false);}
 }
};
// Áudio local gerado com WebAudio: música curta em loop só quando usuário habilitar, sem downloads externos.
let v32AudioTimer=null,v32AudioStep=0;
function v32StopMusic(){if(v32AudioTimer){clearInterval(v32AudioTimer);v32AudioTimer=null;}}
function v32Note(frequency,duration=.13){if(!soundEnabled||save.v32.volume<=0||document.hidden)return;
 try{audioCtx=audioCtx||new(window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();if(audioCtx.state==='suspended')audioCtx.resume();
  const now=audioCtx.currentTime,osc=audioCtx.createOscillator(),gain=audioCtx.createGain();osc.type='triangle';
  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency,now);gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,now);gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(save.v32.volume*.00028,now+.015);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,now+duration);
  osc.connect(gain).connect(audioCtx.destination);osc.start(now);osc.stop(now+duration+.01);
 }catch(e){console.warn('V32 som indisponível:',e);v32StopMusic();}
}
function v32RefreshAudio(){v32StopMusic();if(!soundEnabled||!save.v32.volume)return;
 const melody=[329.6,392,440,392,293.7,349.2,392,293.7,329.6,392,523.25,493.9,440,392,329.6,293.7];
 v32AudioTimer=setInterval(()=>{v32Note(melody[v32AudioStep++%melody.length],.19)},500);
}
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)v32StopMusic();else if(soundEnabled)v32RefreshAudio()});

Controle o volume ou desligue sem afetar o combate.</p><div class="v32SoundControl"><button class="btn ${soundEnabled?'gold':'ghost'}" id="v32SoundToggle">${soundEnabled?'🔊 Desligar':'🔇 Ligar'} áudio</button><label for="v32SoundRange">Volume <b id="v32SoundLevel">${save.v32.volume}%</b></label><input type="range" min="0" max="100" id="v32SoundRange" value="${save.v32.volume}"></div><button class="btn ghost wide" id="v32SoundReturn">Voltar</button>`);

// Personalidade Veloz: deslocamento um pouco mais rápido, sem afetar a mira nem teleporte.
const v32UpdateOld=BattleEngine.prototype.update;
BattleEngine.prototype.update=function(dt,now){
 const can=!!(battle?.active&&!battle.paused&&this.player&&current()?.v32Nature==='veloz');
 const x=this.player?.x||0,y=this.player?.y||0;
 const result=v32UpdateOld.call(this,dt,now);
 if(can&&battle?.active&&!battle.paused&&this.player){
  this.player.x+=(this.player.x-x)*.07;this.player.y+=(this.player.y-y)*.07;
  if(typeof this.collideRocks==='function')this.collideRocks(this.player);
  if(typeof this.bounds==='function')this.bounds(this.player);
 }
 return result;
};

BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){
 const s=battle?.active?skillSet(current())[i]:null;
 const now=performance.now(),can=!!(s&&battle&&!battle.paused&&cooldowns[i]<=now&&battle.energy>=s.cost);
 const result=v32BattleSoundOld.call(this,i);
 if(can&&soundEnabled&&s){const sounds={electric:659,psychic:520,water:392,fire:261.6,ground:196,rock:220,ice:784,poison:293.7,grass:349.2};v32Note(sounds[s.type]||440,.09);}
 return result;
};

const V33_FX_COLORS={fire:'#ff934c',water:'#64c4ff',electric:'#ffdf62',grass:'#9cdf80',psychic:'#dca0ff',ground:'#d6a472',rock:'#c7b693',poison:'#d7a2ef',ice:'#b3f9ff',ghost:'#a3b5ef',fighting:'#ffc49a',normal:'#dde8ff',bug:'#b3d07c',dragon:'#b29bfc'};
const v33SafeNumber=(x,fallback)=>Number.isFinite(+x)?+x:fallback;
function v33Ensure(){
 if(!save.v33||typeof save.v33!=='object')save.v33={};
 const s=save.v33;
 if(!s.stats||typeof s.stats!=='object')s.stats={fights:0,wins:0,losses:0,captures:0,shiny:0,damage:0,misses:0,moves:{},legendary:[],bestCombo:0};
 if(!Array.isArray(s.stats.legendary))s.stats.legendary=[];
 if(!s.stats.moves||typeof s.stats.moves!=='object')s.stats.moves={};
 if(!Array.isArray(s.discovered))s.discovered=[];
 if(!s.settings||typeof s.settings!=='object')s.settings={effects:true,performance:'auto'};
 for(const m of [...(save.team||[]),...(save.box||[])]){
  if(!m||!D[m.id])continue;
  if(!m.v33Train||typeof m.v33Train!=='object')m.v33Train={atk:0,def:0,spd:0,special:0};
  for(const stat of ['atk','def','spd','special'])m.v33Train[stat]=Math.max(0,Math.min(8,Math.floor(v33SafeNumber(m.v33Train[stat],0))));
 }
 return s;
}

const v33MoveForSpecies=(m)=>{
 const row=V33_SPECIES_MOVES[m?.id];if(!row)return null;
 const [name,type,kind,power,level]=row;
 return {name,type,kind,power,cat:power?'special':'status',icon:V32_ADD_MOVES[type]?.icon||'✨',cost:power===0?24:Math.min(49,Math.round(power*.67)),cd:kind==='homing'?10000:kind==='area'?4500:kind==='beam'?3900:kind==='recover'?9200:kind==='guard'?6500:kind==='illusion'?8500:kind==='status'?6800:2300,level};
};

const v33CataloguePrev=v32Catalogue;
v32Catalogue=function(m){
 const all=v33CataloguePrev(m),special=v33MoveForSpecies(m);
 if(!special)return all;
 const key=v32MoveKey(special);
 if(!all.some(x=>x.key===key))all.push({key,move:special,level:special.level,tm:false,available:m.level>=special.level});
 return all;
};
const v33SkillPrev=skillSet;
skillSet=function(m){const s=v33SkillPrev(m);if(!m||!D[m.id])return s;
 const special=v33MoveForSpecies(m);
 if(special&&m.level>=special.level&&!Array.isArray(m.v32Slots)){
  const slot=(m.id===143||m.id===150||m.id===149)?2:1;
  if(s[slot]&&s[slot].name!==special.name)s[slot]={...special};
 }
 for(const move of s)if(move?.kind==='homing')move.cd=Math.max(10000,move.cd||0);
 return s;
};
function v33Toast(message){const prev=document.querySelector('.v33Message');prev?.remove();const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='v31Message v33Message';d.textContent=message;document.body.append(d);setTimeout(()=>d.remove(),2300);}
function v33Track(name,damageValue=0){const info=v33Ensure().stats;info.moves[name]=(info.moves[name]||0)+1;info.damage+=Math.max(0,Math.round(damageValue));}
const v33PersistPrev=persist;
persist=function(){v33Ensure();return v33PersistPrev();};
const v33DamagePrev=damage;
damage=function(att,def,type,power){const r=v33DamagePrev(att,def,type,power);if(!r||r.hit<=0)return r;
 const a=att?.v33Train||{},d=def?.v33Train||{};
 let factor=(1+Math.min(8,a.atk||0)*.018+Math.min(8,a.special||0)*.009)/(1+Math.min(8,d.def||0)*.018);
 const t=battle?.v33Terrain;
 // O bônus do cenário é centralizado na V35.35, sem multiplicação antiga duplicada.
 r.hit=Math.max(1,Math.round(r.hit*factor));return r;
};
const v33StartPrev=startBattle;

 const b=battle;
 b.v33Tracker={moves:{},damage:0,misses:0};
 b.v33Terrain=(wild?'grass':save.gym===0?'rock':save.gym===1?'water':save.gym===2?'electric':save.gym===3?'grass':save.gym===5?'psychic':save.gym===7?'rock':'normal');
 b.v33StartTime=performance.now();b.v33Type='normal';
 b.v33Boss=(!wild&&save.gym>=0&&save.gym<=7&&(/LÍDER/i.test(label||''))&&!!V33_LEADERS[save.gym])?save.gym:-1;
 b.v33BossStage=0;b.v33LastEnemyHp=b.foe?.hp||0;
 if(engine){engine.v33Fx=[];engine.v33FrameWarn=false;}
 v33BattleTools();
};
function v33BattleTools(){if(!battle?.active)return;const arena=$('arenaPro');if(!arena)return;

 if(battle.v33Boss>=0){let boss=$('v33BossStyle');if(!boss){boss=document.createElement('div');boss.id='v33BossStyle';boss.className='v33Trainer';arena.append(boss);}boss.textContent=V33_LEADERS[battle.v33Boss].style;}
 const d=battle.v31Duo;if(!d)return;
 let box=$('v33DoubleControls');if(box)return;
 box=document.createElement('div');box.id='v33DoubleControls';box.className='v33DuoControls';
 box.innerHTML='<button type="button" id="v33TargetPrimary">🎯 Alvo A</button><button type="button" id="v33TargetPartner">🎯 Alvo B</button><button type="button" id="v33SupportAttack">⚔️ Golpe parceiro</button><button type="button" id="v33SupportHeal">💚 Apoio</button><button type="button" id="v33SupportSwap">🔁 Trocar parceiro</button>';
 arena.append(box);
 d.v33Manual=true;d.v33Target='main';d.v33SupportCD=0;d.v33HealCD=0;
 const update=()=>{if(!battle?.active||battle.v31Duo!==d)return;
  const main=$('v33TargetPrimary'),p=$('v33TargetPartner');if(main)main.style.borderColor=d.v33Target==='main'?'#d4cc72':'#65a5ae';if(p)p.style.borderColor=d.v33Target==='partner'?'#d4cc72':'#65a5ae';
  if(p)p.disabled=!(d.enemy&&d.enemy.hp>0);
  const attack=$('v33SupportAttack'),heal=$('v33SupportHeal');if(attack)attack.disabled=!(d.ally&&d.ally.hp>0);if(heal)heal.disabled=!(d.ally&&d.ally.hp>0);
 };
 $('v33TargetPrimary').onclick=()=>{d.v33Target='main';update()};
 $('v33TargetPartner').onclick=()=>{d.v33Target='partner';update()};
 $('v33SupportAttack').onclick=()=>{
  if(!battle?.active||battle.paused||d.ally.hp<=0||performance.now()<d.v33SupportCD)return;
  const enemy=d.v33Target==='partner'&&d.enemy?.hp>0?d.enemy:foe();if(!enemy||enemy.hp<=0)return;
  d.v33SupportCD=performance.now()+2250;
  const move=skillSet(d.ally)[1]||skillSet(d.ally)[0];const power=Math.min(52,move.power||23),hit=damage(d.ally,enemy,move.type,power).hit;
  enemy.hp=Math.max(0,enemy.hp-hit);engine?.particlesAt(engine.enemy.x,engine.enemy.y,V33_FX_COLORS[move.type]||'#d8fdff',9,70);
  engine?.damageLabels.push({x:engine.enemy.x+(enemy===d.enemy?30:0),y:engine.enemy.y-30,text:'PARCEIRO -'+hit,life:.8,color:'#b4ffe8'});
  if(enemy===d.enemy&&enemy.hp<=0){d.enemy=null;d.v33Target='main';}
  else if(enemy===foe()&&enemy.hp<=0){nextFoe();}
  update();
 };
 $('v33SupportHeal').onclick=()=>{
  if(!battle?.active||battle.paused||d.ally.hp<=0||performance.now()<d.v33HealCD||battle.energy<22)return;
  d.v33HealCD=performance.now()+8200;battle.energy-=22;const target=d.ally.hp<current().hp?d.ally:current();

// Na V31 o ataque do parceiro era automático. Na V33, o jogador controla os comandos de apoio.
const v33UpdatePrev=BattleEngine.prototype.update;
BattleEngine.prototype.update=function(dt,now){
 const b=battle,duo=b?.v31Duo;
 if(duo?.v33Manual){duo.clockAlly=99999;}
 const originalX=this.player?.x||0,originalY=this.player?.y||0;
 const result=v33UpdatePrev.call(this,dt,now);
 if(!battle?.active||battle.paused)return result;
 if(duo?.v33Manual){duo.clockAlly=99999;if(duo.enemy?.hp<=0){duo.enemy=null;duo.v33Target='main';}}
 if(battle.v33Boss>=0){
  const g=battle.v33Boss,profile=V33_LEADERS[g];
  if(profile){this.enemyAttackCD=Math.max(.25,Math.min(12,this.enemyAttackCD+dt*(profile.tempo-1)*.7));
   if(Number.isFinite(this.foeDodgeCD))this.foeDodgeCD=Math.max(.13,this.foeDodgeCD+dt*(profile.evade-1)*.5);
   const f=foe();if(f?.hp>0&&f.hp<=f.maxHp*.42&&battle.v33BossStage<1){
    battle.v33BossStage=1;this.enemyAttackCD=Math.max(.25,this.enemyAttackCD-.35);
    engine?.particlesAt(this.enemy.x,this.enemy.y,V33_FX_COLORS[profile.type]||'#fff',17,78);
    v33Toast('👑 '+profile.name+': "Agora é minha vez de lutar a sério!"');
   }
  }
 }
 // Treinamento leve de velocidade mantém o personagem dentro da arena.
 const sp=Math.min(8,current()?.v33Train?.spd||0);
 if(sp>0&&this.player){this.player.x+=(this.player.x-originalX)*sp*.004;this.player.y+=(this.player.y-originalY)*sp*.004;this.bounds(this.player);}
 return result;
};
const v33HitPrev=BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget;
BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget=function(who,type,power){
 if(!battle?.active)return;
 const p=who==='enemy'?current():foe(),a=who==='enemy'?foe():current();
 const aSpeed=(D[a.id]?.spd||50)+Math.min(8,a.v33Train?.spd||0)*3;
 const dSpeed=(D[p.id]?.spd||50)+Math.min(8,p.v33Train?.spd||0)*3;
 const evade=Math.max(.035,Math.min(.14,.045+(dSpeed-aSpeed)*.00035));
 if(Math.random()<evade){
  const target=who==='enemy'?this.enemy:this.player;
  if(target){this.damageLabels.push({x:target.x,y:target.y-34,text:'ESQUIVOU',life:.7,color:'#c9f1ff'});this.particlesAt(target.x,target.y,'#cee6ff',5,45);}
  v33Ensure().stats.misses++;
  if(battle.v33Tracker)battle.v33Tracker.misses++;
  return;
 }
 if(who==='enemy'&&battle.v31Duo?.v33Manual&&battle.v31Duo.v33Target==='partner'&&battle.v31Duo.enemy?.hp>0){
  const enemy=battle.v31Duo.enemy,hit=damage(current(),enemy,type,power).hit;
  enemy.hp=Math.max(0,enemy.hp-hit);
  this.damageLabels.push({x:this.enemy.x+35,y:this.enemy.y-31,text:'- '+hit,life:.7,color:'#ffe4a0'});
  this.particlesAt(this.enemy.x+30,this.enemy.y,V33_FX_COLORS[type]||'#fff',10,75);
  if(battle.v33Tracker)battle.v33Tracker.damage+=hit;
  if(enemy.hp===0){battle.v31Duo.enemy=null;battle.v31Duo.v33Target='main';v33Toast('Alvo secundário derrotado!');}
  return;
 }
 const hp=p.hp;
 const r=v33HitPrev.call(this,who,type,power);
 if(who==='enemy'&&battle?.v33Tracker)battle.v33Tracker.damage+=Math.max(0,hp-p.hp);
 if(battle?.active&&battle.v33Fx){ /* reserve for visual effects */ }
 return r;
};
const v33CastPrev=BattleEngine.prototype.useMove;
BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){
 if(!battle?.active||battle.paused)return v33CastPrev.call(this,i);
 const m=current(),s=skillSet(m)[i],now=performance.now();
 if(!s||cooldowns[i]>now||battle.energy<s.cost||m.hp<=0)return;
 if(this.v33Windup&&now<this.v33Windup.until)return;
 const name=s.name;
 if(battle.v33Tracker)battle.v33Tracker.moves[name]=(battle.v33Tracker.moves[name]||0)+1;
 v33Ensure().stats.moves[name]=(save.v33.stats.moves[name]||0)+1;
 const fx={type:s.type,start:now,until:now+260,from:'player'};
 if(!this.v33Fx)this.v33Fx=[];this.v33Fx.push(fx);if(this.v33Fx.length>12)this.v33Fx.shift();
 const castHeavy=(s.power>=60||V33_HEAVY.has(name))&&s.kind!=='homing';
 if(castHeavy){
  const b=battle,me=m;this.v33Windup={until:now+410};
  let chip=$('v33CastCall');if(!chip){chip=document.createElement('div');chip.id='v33CastCall';chip.className='v33BattleCall';$('arenaPro')?.append(chip);}chip.textContent='✨ '+name+' · Preparando...';
  setTimeout(()=>{if(this.v33Windup)this.v33Windup=null;chip?.remove();if(battle===b&&b.active&&!b.paused&&current()===me){v33CastPrev.call(this,i);}},420);
  return;
 }
 return v33CastPrev.call(this,i);
};
const v33DrawPrev=BattleEngine.prototype.draw;
BattleEngine.prototype.draw=function(now){
 v33DrawPrev.call(this,now);
 if(!battle?.active)return;
 const ctx=this.ctx,w=this.w,h=this.h,t=now*.001;
 ctx.save();
 // Props pequenos nas bordas; nunca atrás do Pokémon ou cobrindo barras de vida.
 const terrain=battle.v33Terrain;
 if(terrain!=='normal'){
  const color={rock:'#c9bd96',water:'#6bd7f5',grass:'#83c77b',electric:'#ecd879',psychic:'#c79df3'}[terrain]||'#d5e1e7';
  ctx.globalAlpha=.32;ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.strokeStyle=color;
  for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
   const x=w*(.06+(i*137%77)/100),y=h*(i%2===0?.18:.85),r=3+(i%3)*2;
   if(terrain==='water'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y,r*2.4,r*.55,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();}
   else if(terrain==='grass'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-r,y);ctx.lineTo(x,y-r*1.5);ctx.lineTo(x+r,y);ctx.fill();}
   else if(terrain==='electric'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-r,y-r);ctx.lineTo(x+1,y);ctx.lineTo(x-2,y+r);ctx.stroke();}
   else{ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-r,y+r);ctx.lineTo(x,y-r);ctx.lineTo(x+r,y+r);ctx.closePath();ctx.stroke();}
  }
 }
 // Efeitos 2D leves vinculados ao desenho existente, sem novo RAF.
 if(save.v33?.settings?.effects!==false){
  this.v33Fx=(this.v33Fx||[]).filter(x=>x.until>now);
  for(const f of this.v33Fx){
   const life=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(f.until-now)/260)),cx=f.from==='player'?this.player.x:this.enemy.x,cy=f.from==='player'?this.player.y:this.enemy.y;
   ctx.globalAlpha=.25*life;ctx.strokeStyle=V33_FX_COLORS[f.type]||'#fff';ctx.lineWidth=1.4;
   const lines=6;
   for(let i=0;i<lines;i++){const a=i*Math.PI*2/lines+t*.5,outer=8+20*(1-life);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(cx+Math.cos(a)*8,cy+Math.sin(a)*8);ctx.lineTo(cx+Math.cos(a+.13)*outer,cy+Math.sin(a+.13)*outer);ctx.stroke();}
  }
 }
 ctx.restore();
};
const v33RenderBattlePrev=renderBattle;
renderBattle=function(){v33RenderBattlePrev();if(battle?.active)v33BattleTools();};
function v33Result(title,message,win=true){
 if(!battle?.active)return;
 battle.active=false;stopTimers();battle=null;
 resultScreen(title,message,win,false);
}
const v33FinishPrev=finishBattle;
finishBattle=function(win){
 if(!battle?.active)return;
 const b=battle,mode=b.v33Type;
 const stats=v33Ensure().stats;
 if(mode!=='lab'){stats.fights++;if(win)stats.wins++;else stats.losses++;}else{stats.lab=(stats.lab||0)+1;}
 if(b.v33Tracker){stats.damage+=b.v33Tracker.damage;}
 if(mode==='lab'){
  const snapshot=b.v33Snapshot||[];
  for(const state of snapshot){const m=save.team.find(p=>p===state.ref);if(m)Object.assign(m,state.data);}
  if(b.v33SaveSnapshot){save.coins=b.v33SaveSnapshot.coins;save.gym=b.v33SaveSnapshot.gym;save.stage=b.v33SaveSnapshot.stage;save.wins=b.v33SaveSnapshot.wins;save.losses=b.v33SaveSnapshot.losses;if(Array.isArray(b.v33SaveSnapshot.seen))save.seen=b.v33SaveSnapshot.seen;if(Array.isArray(b.v33SaveSnapshot.caught))save.caught=b.v33SaveSnapshot.caught;}
  persist();v33Result('🧪 LABORATÓRIO · '+(win?'VITÓRIA':'DERROTA'),'Seu time foi restaurado. Insígnias, moedas e campanha não foram alteradas.',win);return;
 }
 if(mode==='legendary'){
  if(win){const legend=b.v33Legendary;save.coins+=65;
   v33Result('✨ DESAFIO LENDÁRIO VENCIDO!',legend+' recuou! Tente uma Pokébola durante o combate para capturá-lo. Bônus: 65 moedas.',true);persist();}
  else{persist();v33Result('⚡ DESAFIO LENDÁRIO','A batalha foi difícil. Treine seu time e tente novamente.',false);}return;
 }
 persist();return v33FinishPrev(win);
};
const v33CapturePrev=finishCapture;
finishCapture=function(m){
 const legendary=battle?.v33Type==='legendary';
 if(battle?.active){const stats=v33Ensure().stats;stats.captures++;if(m?.shiny)stats.shiny++;if(legendary&&!stats.legendary.includes(m.id))stats.legendary.push(m.id);persist();}
 return v33CapturePrev(m);
};

function v33TrainMon(m){if(!m||!save.team.includes(m)||battle?.active)return;

Limite: 8 por atributo.</p><div class="v33Pane">🪙 Moedas: <b>'+save.coins+'</b> · Nível '+m.level+'</div><div id="v33TrainCards">'+Object.entries(labels).map(([key,label])=>{
 const lv=tr[key]||0,cost=25+lv*16;
 return '<div class="v33Pane"><strong>'+label+'</strong><small> · '+lv+'/8</small><div class="v33Bar"><i style="width:'+(lv*12.5)+'%"></i></div><button class="btn tiny gold" style="margin-top:8px" data-v33-train="'+key+'" '+(lv>=8||save.coins<cost?'disabled':'')+'>'+(lv>=8?'MÁXIMO':'🪙 '+cost+' · Treinar')+'</button></div>';
 }).join('')+'</div>');
 $('v33TrainBack').onclick=v33TrainingMenu;
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v33-train]').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>{
  const stat=btn.dataset.v33Train,lv=tr[stat];if(!Number.isInteger(lv)||lv>=8)return;const cost=25+lv*16;if(save.coins<cost)return;
  save.coins-=cost;tr[stat]=lv+1;persist();v33TrainMon(m);
 });
}

 function v331AsMove(row,type){
  const [name,kind,level,power]=row,heavy=power>=60;
  const cost=kind==='shield'||kind==='recover'?24:kind==='dash'?Math.max(0,Math.round((power-26)*.55)):Math.round(power*.58);
  const cd=kind==='homing'?10000:kind==='area'?4600:kind==='hazard'?6300:kind==='pierce'?3200:kind==='beam'?heavy?6800:4000:kind==='recover'?8500:kind==='shield'?5800:kind==='dash'?1400:2300;
  return {name,type,kind,cat:power?'special':'status',power,level,cost:Math.min(55,cost),cd,icon:V32_ADD_MOVES[type]?.icon||'✨'};
 }
 v32Catalogue=function(m){
  if(!m||!D[m.id])return [];
  const base=v331PreviousCatalogue(m),p=D[m.id],types=[...new Set([p.type,p.type2].filter(Boolean))];
  const keys=new Set(base.map(x=>x.key));
  const restricted=V331_SMALL_CATALOG[m.id];
  for(const t of [...new Set(['normal',...types])]){
   for(const row of MOVE_DEFS[t]||[]){
    if(restricted && !restricted.includes(row[0]))continue;
    const move=v331AsMove(row,t),key=v331Key(move);
    if(keys.has(key))continue;keys.add(key);
    base.push({key,move,level:move.level,tm:false,available:m.level>=move.level});
   }
  }
  // A assinatura da espécie continua disponível; a quantidade de opções cresce com o nível.
  return base.sort((a,b)=>(a.level||1)-(b.level||1)||a.move.name.localeCompare(b.move.name,'pt'));
 };

 function v331MovesEditor(m){if(!m||battle?.active)return;
  const all=[...save.team,...save.box];if(!all.includes(m))return;
  let slot=0,filter='available';
  function paint(){
   const cat=v32Catalogue(m),defaults=v32DefaultSlots(m);
   const selected=(Array.isArray(m.v32Slots)&&m.v32Slots.length===4?m.v32Slots:defaults).slice();

   const available=cat.filter(x=>filter==='all'||(filter==='available'?x.available:!x.available));
   const controls=chosen.map((x,i)=>'<button class="v331Slot '+(slot===i?'active':'')+'" data-v331-slot="'+i+'"><b>'+(i+1)+'. '+(x?.move.icon||'✦')+' '+(x?.move.name||'Golpe')+'</b><small>'+(x?.move.cost||0)+' energia · '+(((x?.move.cd||1000)/1000).toFixed(1))+'s</small></button>').join('');
   const moves=available.map(x=>'<button class="v331Choice '+(selected.includes(x.key)?'equipped':'')+'" data-v331-pick="'+cat.indexOf(x)+'" '+(x.available?'':'disabled')+'><span><b>'+(x.move.icon||'✦')+' '+x.move.name+'</b><small>'+(TYPE_NAME[x.move.type]||x.move.type)+' · '+(x.move.power||'Defesa')+' força · '+x.move.cost+' energia · '+(x.move.cd/1000).toFixed(1)+'s</small></span><span class="pillSmall">'+(selected.includes(x.key)?'EM USO':x.available?'EQUIPAR':'Nv. '+x.level)+'</span></button>').join('');
   overlay('<div class="sectionhead"><div><div class="overline">PERSONALIZAR · '+D[m.id].name+'</div><h2>🧠 '+D[m.id].name+' · Nv. '+m.level+'</h2></div><button class="btn tiny ghost" id="v331BackMoves">✕</button></div><p class="desc">1. Escolha um dos quatro espaços. 2. Toque no novo golpe. A mudança é salva automaticamente.</p><div class="v331Slots">'+controls+'</div><div class="v331Tabs"><button class="btn tiny '+(filter==='available'?'gold':'ghost')+'" data-v331-filter="available">Liberados ('+cat.filter(x=>x.available).length+')</button><button class="btn tiny '+(filter==='all'?'gold':'ghost')+'" data-v331-filter="all">Todos ('+cat.length+')</button><button class="btn tiny '+(filter==='locked'?'gold':'ghost')+'" data-v331-filter="locked">Futuros</button></div><div class="v331MoveList">'+moves+'</div><div class="v331Actions"><button class="btn ghost" id="v331DefaultMoves">↻ Restaurar golpes</button><button class="btn gold" id="v331Done">✓ Pronto</button></div><div class="v331Feedback" id="v331Feedback" aria-live="polite"></div>');
   $('v331BackMoves').onclick=v331ChoosePokemon;$('v331Done').onclick=showTeam;
   $('v331DefaultMoves').onclick=()=>{m.v32Slots=null;persist();paint()};
   document.querySelectorAll('[data-v331-slot]').forEach(el=>el.onclick=()=>{slot=+el.dataset.v331Slot;paint()});
   document.querySelectorAll('[data-v331-filter]').forEach(el=>el.onclick=()=>{filter=el.dataset.v331Filter;paint()});
   document.querySelectorAll('[data-v331-pick]').forEach(el=>el.onclick=()=>{
    const x=cat[+el.dataset.v331Pick];if(!x||!x.available)return;
    const list=selected.slice();if(list.some((key,i)=>key===x.key && i!==slot)){const note=$('v331Feedback');if(note)note.textContent='Este golpe já ocupa outra posição.';return;}
    list[slot]=x.key;m.v32Slots=list;persist();
    const selectedNow=skillSet(m)[slot];
    if(selectedNow?.name!==x.move.name){m.v32Slots=selected;persist();const note=$('v331Feedback');if(note)note.textContent='Não foi possível equipar '+x.move.name;return;}
    paint();const note=$('v331Feedback');if(note)note.textContent='✓ '+x.move.name+' equipado e salvo na posição '+(slot+1);
   });
  }
  paint();
 }

 function v331TrainerInfo(){
  const b=battle;if(!b?.active||b.wild)return null;
  const name=b.label||'';if(/LABORATÓRIO|LENDÁRIO|TORRE|REVANCHE DA LIGA/i.test(name))return null;
  if(/LÍDER\s/i.test(name) && save.gym<8)return {title:GYMS[save.gym].leader,src:V27_TRAINER_GIFS[GYMS[save.gym].leader]||'',leader:true};
  if(/ROCKET/i.test(name))return {title:'Recruta Rocket',src:'https://play.pokemonshowdown.com/sprites/trainers/rocketgrunt.png'};
  if(/RIVAL/i.test(name))return {title:'Rival Blue',src:'https://play.pokemonshowdown.com/sprites/trainers/blue-gen1.png'};
  const list=V331_TRAINERS[save.gym]||V331_TRAINERS[0],step=Math.max(0,save.stage||0),x=list[step%list.length];
  return {title:x[0]+' de Kanto',src:'https://play.pokemonshowdown.com/sprites/trainers/'+x[1]+'.png'};
 }

 }

 const oldUseMove=BattleEngine.prototype.useMove;
 function v331OverlayFeedback(msg){const line=$('v331BattleNotice');if(line){line.textContent=msg;return;}const parent=document.querySelector('.battleFooter');if(!parent)return;const el=document.createElement('span');el.id='v331BattleNotice';el.className='v331BattleNotice';el.textContent=msg;parent.prepend(el);setTimeout(()=>el.remove(),1600);}
 BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){
  const b=battle;if(!b?.active||i<0||i>3||!Number.isInteger(i))return;
  const monNow=current(),s=skillSet(monNow)[i],now=performance.now();if(!s)return;
  if(b.paused){
   if(b.v31Duo && this.running && (this.v25?.entry?.length||this.v301Faint) && !this.v25?.capture){this.v331Queued={slot:i,b};v331OverlayFeedback('Pokémon entrando! Golpe '+(i+1)+' na fila.');return;}
   if(b.v31Duo && this.running && !this.v25?.entry?.length && !this.v25?.capture && !b.v301Fainting && $('swapSheet')?.classList.contains('hide'))b.paused=false;
   else{v331OverlayFeedback('Aguarde a animação de entrada para atacar.');return;}
  }
  if(cooldowns[i]>now){v331OverlayFeedback(s.name+' recarregando.');return;}
  if(b.energy<s.cost){v331OverlayFeedback('Energia insuficiente para '+s.name+'.');return;}
  if(monNow.hp<=0)return;
  if(this.v33Windup&&now<this.v33Windup.until){v331OverlayFeedback('Preparando o golpe anterior.');return;}
  // O renderizador antigo só reconhecia projéteis teleguiados e áreas na posição 3.
  // Agora qualquer um dos quatro slots pode executar esses golpes com a mesma mecânica 2D.
  if(['homing','pierce','area','hazard'].includes(s.kind) && (i!==2 || !V30_MOVE_TRAITS[monNow.id])){
   b.energy-=s.cost;cooldowns[i]=now+(s.kind==='homing'?Math.max(10000,s.cd):s.cd);
   this.attackActs.player=this.time+.45;this.v30Shots ||= [];this.v30Zones ||= [];
   const aim=this.aimVector(),x=this.player.x,y=this.player.y,color=TYPE_COLOR[s.type]||'#c6e4ff';
   this.particlesAt(x,y,color,13,88);
   if(s.kind==='homing'||s.kind==='pierce'){
    this.v30Shots.push({x,y,vx:aim.x*this.h*.60,vy:aim.y*this.h*.60,kind:s.kind,type:s.type,power:s.power,life:2.2,trail:[],hit:false});this.v30Shots=this.v30Shots.slice(-12);
   } else {
    this.v30Zones.push({kind:s.kind,x:this.enemy.x,y:this.enemy.y,type:s.type,power:s.power,life:s.kind==='hazard'?3.1:.42,tick:.4,r:this.h*.21,hit:false});this.v30Zones=this.v30Zones.slice(-8);
   }
   if(typeof v27EmitFx==='function')v27EmitFx(s.type,'enemy',1.1);
   log(D[monNow.id].name+' usou '+s.name+'!');playSound('move');
   if(b.v33Tracker)b.v33Tracker.moves[s.name]=(b.v33Tracker.moves[s.name]||0)+1;
   v33Ensure().stats.moves[s.name]=(save.v33.stats.moves[s.name]||0)+1;
   return;
  }
  if(s.kind==='recover'||s.kind==='illusion'||s.kind==='shield'){
   // Protege todas as posições (o motor anterior só tratava o 4º golpe como status).
   if(s.kind==='shield' && i!==3){b.energy-=s.cost;cooldowns[i]=now+s.cd;this.player.guard=1.9;this.rings.push({x:this.player.x,y:this.player.y,r:12,life:1.3,color:'#9cdbff',follow:'player'});log(monNow.id+' usou '+s.name);return;}
  }
  return oldUseMove.call(this,i);
 };
 const oldUpdate=BattleEngine.prototype.update;
 BattleEngine.prototype.update=function(dt,now){
  const outcome=oldUpdate.call(this,dt,now);
  const queued=this.v331Queued;
  if(queued && battle===queued.b && queued.b.active && !battle.paused && this.running){this.v331Queued=null;this.useMove(queued.slot);}
  if(queued && (!battle?.active||battle!==queued.b))this.v331Queued=null;
  return outcome;
 };

 window.PokemonTorreV331Diagnostics=()=>({version:V331,trainer:v331TrainerInfo()?.title||null,catalog:save.team[0]?v32Catalogue(save.team[0]).map(x=>({name:x.move.name,key:x.key,available:x.available})):[],team:save.team.map(m=>({id:m.id,skills:skillSet(m).map(x=>x.name),catalogue:v32Catalogue(m).length,available:v32Catalogue(m).filter(x=>x.available).length})),duoActive:!!battle?.v31Duo,queued:!!engine?.v331Queued});

const aliases={'Lança-Chamas':'Flamethrower','Raio':'Thunderbolt','Confusion':'Confusion','Hidro Bomba':'Hydro Pump','Terremoto':'Earthquake','Surf':'Surf','Psíquico':'Psychic','Slam':'Body Slam'};
const v34CatalogPrev=v32Catalogue;
function asMove(name,type,lvl){
 const t=moveTraits[name]||[type,(name.includes('Beam')||name.includes('Raio')?'beam':name.includes('Spin')?'hazard':'bolt'),32+Math.min(19,lvl*.22),2600+Math.min(1800,lvl*11),23+Math.min(16,lvl*.2)];
 return {name,type:t[0],kind:t[1],power:t[2],cd:t[3],cost:t[4],cat:t[2]>0?'special':'status',icon:(V32_ADD_MOVES[t[0]]?.icon)||'✨'};
}
// Não remove os golpes já equipados das versões anteriores.
v32Catalogue=function(m){
 const existing=v34CatalogPrev(m);if(!m||!D[m.id])return existing;
 const seen=new Set(existing.map(x=>x.key));const d=D[m.id];const names=[...(species[m.id]||[])];
 for(const t of [d.type,d.type2])if(t)names.push(...(typeMoves[t]||[]));
 const add=(name,n)=>{const same=existing.find(x=>x.move.name===name||aliases[x.move.name]===name);if(same)return;
  const type=Object.entries(typeMoves).find(([,ns])=>ns.includes(name))?.[0]||
    (Object.entries(moveTraits).find(([nm])=>nm===name)?.[1]?.[0])||d.type;
  const level=clamp(5+Math.floor(n/2)*7,5,78),move=asMove(name,type,level),key=v32MoveKey(move);
  if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);existing.push({key,move,level,tm:false,available:m.level>=level});};
 names.forEach(add);
 if(m.id===105){
   let eq=existing.find(x=>x.move?.name==='Earthquake');
   if(!eq){const move=asMove('Earthquake','ground',1),key=v32MoveKey(move);
     existing.push({key,move,level:1,tm:false,available:true});
   }else{eq.level=1;eq.available=true;}
 }
 // Para Pokémon da mesma linhagem, os conjuntos-base antigos permanecem disponíveis.
 return existing;
};

 $('v34Close').onclick=prevTeam;$('v34TeamBack').onclick=showTeam;$('v34Compare').onclick=()=>profile(m,true);$('v34MoveBack').onclick=()=>{showTeam();const editor=$('v331MovesShortcut');if(editor)editor.click();};
}

// 10: volta ao motor de ataque automático já presente no V31, sem duplicar loops.
function setAutoDuo(){const duo=battle?.v31Duo;if(!duo)return;
 duo.v33Manual=false;duo.v33Target='main';if(!Number.isFinite(duo.clockAlly)||duo.clockAlly>100)duo.clockAlly=.7;
 const old=$('v33DoubleControls');if(old)old.remove();$('v331DuoNotice')?.remove();let tag=$('v34DuoChip');if(!tag){tag=document.createElement('div');tag.className='v34AutoDuo';tag.id='v34DuoChip';const footer=document.querySelector('.battleFooter');footer?.querySelector('.skillgridV8')?.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',tag);}
 if(tag)tag.textContent='⚔️ '+mvName(duo.ally)+' ataca automaticamente · Você controla o Pokémon principal';
}
const prevStart=startBattle;
startBattle=function(enemies,label,wild){const r=prevStart(enemies,label,wild);if(battle?.active){setAutoDuo();battle.v34={start:performance.now(),hits:0,dodges:0,combo:0,lastAttack:null,rewarded:false,highestHp:current()?.hp||0};}return r;};
const prevUpdate=BattleEngine.prototype.update;
BattleEngine.prototype.update=function(dt,now){const b=battle,duo=b?.v31Duo;
 if(duo?.v33Manual){duo.v33Manual=false;if(duo.clockAlly>100)duo.clockAlly=.7;}
 const r=prevUpdate.call(this,dt,now);
 if(!battle?.active||!battle.v34||battle.paused)return r;
 const a=battle.v34;
 if(duo){if(duo.enemy?.hp<=0){duo.enemy=null;duo.v33Target='main';}if(!$('v34DuoChip')&&$('arenaPro'))setAutoDuo();}
 // 3: posicionamento por tipo — velocidade moderada e longe dos HUDs.
 const enemy=foe();if(enemy&&this.enemy&&this.player){const t=D[enemy.id]?.type;
  const rx=this.enemy.x-this.player.x,ry=this.enemy.y-this.player.y,dist=Math.hypot(rx,ry)||1;
  if(['psychic','ghost','water'].includes(t)&&dist<this.h*.22){this.enemy.x+=rx/dist*dt*12;this.enemy.y+=ry/dist*dt*9;}
  if(['fighting','ground','rock'].includes(t)&&dist>this.h*.29){this.enemy.x-=rx/dist*dt*10;this.enemy.y-=ry/dist*dt*7;}
  if(t==='electric'&&dist<this.h*.31){this.enemy.x+=Math.sin(now/500)*dt*9;}
  if(typeof this.bounds==='function')this.bounds(this.enemy);
 }
 // 6: chefe avisa o ataque carregado; sem novo timer de animação.
 if(battle.v33Boss>=0){
  if(!a.nextBoss)a.nextBoss=now+8500;
  if(!a.cast&&now>=a.nextBoss){a.cast={end:now+1050,type:V33_LEADERS[battle.v33Boss].type};a.nextBoss=now+9300;
   let chip=document.createElement('div');chip.className='v34Callout';chip.id='v34BossCast';chip.textContent='⚠️ '+V33_LEADERS[battle.v33Boss].name+' prepara um golpe especial!';$('arenaPro')?.append(chip);
  }
  if(a.cast&&now>=a.cast.end){const cast=a.cast;a.cast=null;$('v34BossCast')?.remove();if(battle?.active&&this.player&&this.enemy){
   const near=Math.hypot(this.player.x-this.enemy.x,this.player.y-this.enemy.y)<this.h*.64;
   if(near){this.hitTarget('enemy',cast.type,33);this.particlesAt(this.player.x,this.player.y,'#ffe39b',9,48);}
  }}
 }
 return r;};
// 2: efeitos ligados ao frame existente, limitados e sem alterar o tamanho dos sprites.
const prevDraw=BattleEngine.prototype.draw;
BattleEngine.prototype.draw=function(now){prevDraw.call(this,now);if(!battle?.active||!battle.v34||!this.ctx)return;
 const x=this.ctx,a=battle.v34,fx=a.fx;if(!fx||now-fx.at>480)return;
 let quality=state().settings.quality==='low'||(this.fps>0&&this.fps<28)?4:9;
 const progress=clamp((now-fx.at)/480,0,1),cx=fx.x,cy=fx.y;
 x.save();x.strokeStyle=fx.color;x.fillStyle=fx.color;x.globalAlpha=.53*(1-progress);x.lineWidth=2;
 for(let i=0;i<quality;i++){const ang=i*2*Math.PI/quality+fx.at*.003,len=12+progress*27;
  x.beginPath();x.moveTo(cx+Math.cos(ang)*6,cy+Math.sin(ang)*6);
  if(fx.type==='electric'){const mx=cx+Math.cos(ang)*(len*.6),my=cy+Math.sin(ang)*(len*.6);x.lineTo(mx+Math.sin(ang)*5,my);}
  else if(fx.type==='water'){x.arc(cx,cy,Math.max(3,len*.55),ang,ang+.6);}
  else if(fx.type==='ground'){x.lineTo(cx+Math.cos(ang)*len*.6,cy+Math.sin(ang)*len*.6);}
  x.lineTo(cx+Math.cos(ang)*len,cy+Math.sin(ang)*len);x.stroke();
 }
 x.restore();};
const prevUse=BattleEngine.prototype.useMove;
BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){const b=battle,s=b?.active?skillSet(current())[i]:null,now=performance.now(),valid=s&&b&&!b.paused&&current()?.hp>0&&cooldowns[i]<=now&&b.energy>=s.cost;
 if(valid&&b.v34){const a=b.v34;a.lastAttack=s.type;const color=(TYPE_COLOR[s.type]||'#fff');a.fx={type:s.type,color,at:now,x:this.enemy?.x||this.w/2,y:this.enemy?.y||this.h/2};a.hits++;}
 return prevUse.call(this,i);};
// 7: golpe muito forte pode interromper ataque carregado, mas apenas quando acerta.
const prevHit=BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget;
BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget=function(who,type,power){const before=who==='enemy'?current()?.hp:foe()?.hp,b=battle;const result=prevHit.call(this,who,type,power);
 if(!b?.active||battle!==b||!b.v34)return result;
 const after=who==='enemy'?current()?.hp:foe()?.hp;
 if(who!=='enemy'&&Number(before)>Number(after)&&b.v34.cast&&power>=44){b.v34.cast=null;$('v34BossCast')?.remove();v31Toast('⚡ Ataque do líder interrompido!');}
 if(before===after){b.v34.dodges++;}return result;};
// Combos de campo — bônus pequenos, limitados e sem multiplicadores cumulativos permanentes.
const prevDamage=damage;
damage=function(att,def,type,power){const r=prevDamage(att,def,type,power);if(!r?.hit||!battle?.active||!battle.v34)return r;
 const a=battle.v34,now=performance.now();let bonus=1,label='';
 if(type==='water')a.wetUntil=now+4800;
 if(type==='grass')a.grassUntil=now+5200;
 if(type==='ground')a.crackedUntil=now+5500;
 if(type==='electric'&&a.wetUntil>now){bonus=1.12;label='ÁGUA + ELÉTRICO';a.wetUntil=0;}
 else if(type==='fire'&&a.grassUntil>now){bonus=1.10;label='PLANTA + FOGO';a.grassUntil=0;}
 else if(type==='ice'&&a.wetUntil>now){bonus=1.08;label='ÁGUA + GELO';a.wetUntil=0;}
 else if(type==='rock'&&a.crackedUntil>now){bonus=1.11;label='TERRA + PEDRA';a.crackedUntil=0;}
 if(bonus>1){r.hit=Math.max(1,Math.round(r.hit*bonus));a.combo++;if(typeof v31Toast==='function')v31Toast('✦ COMBO '+label+' +'+Math.round((bonus-1)*100)+'%');}
 return r;};
// 8: captura com três balanços, bônus de status do jogo anterior e chance rara de captura crítica.
// Preserve o sorteio original, o Shiny e as Pokébolas das versões anteriores.
const v34CaptureUpdate=BattleEngine.prototype.v25Update;
BattleEngine.prototype.v25Update=function(dt){
 const cap=this.v25?.capture;
 if(cap && !cap.v34Prepared){
  cap.v34Prepared=true;cap.duration=2.85;cap.critical=Math.random()<.035;
  if(cap.critical){state().stats.crits++;if(!cap.success && Math.random()<.55)cap.success=true;}
 }
 return v34CaptureUpdate.call(this,dt);
};
const v34CaptureDraw=BattleEngine.prototype.v25Draw;
BattleEngine.prototype.v25Draw=function(c){
 v34CaptureDraw.call(this,c);
 const cap=this.v25?.capture;if(!cap||!cap.v34Prepared||cap.time<1.42||cap.time>2.76)return;
 const t=clamp((cap.time-1.42)/1.33,0,1),amp=(1-t)*.32;
 const theta=Math.sin(t*Math.PI*6)*amp;
 if(this.v25Ball)this.v25Ball(c,cap.toX,cap.toY+this.h*.035,this.h*.024,theta);
 c.save();c.textAlign='center';c.font='900 '+Math.max(10,this.h*.021)+'px system-ui';c.fillStyle=cap.critical?'#ffe78b':'#d4efff';c.globalAlpha=.65+.35*Math.sin(t*Math.PI);
 c.fillText(cap.critical?'✦ CAPTURA CRÍTICA':'●  ●  ●',cap.toX,cap.toY+this.h*.085);c.restore();
};

// 9: recompensas pequenas por desempenho; nunca em laboratório e nunca duas vezes.
const prevFinish=finishBattle;
finishBattle=function(win){if(!battle?.active)return;const b=battle,a=b.v34,mode=b.v33Type;
 const eligible=!!(win&&a&&mode!=='lab'&&b.v32Mode!=='lab');
 const hits=a?.hits||0,combo=a?.combo||0,dodges=a?.dodges||0,health=current()?.hp||0;
 const bonus=eligible&&!a.rewarded?Math.min(35,(health>0?8:0)+(combo>0?Math.min(12,combo*4):0)+(dodges>=3?10:0)+(hits>=4?5:0)):0;
 if(a)a.rewarded=true;
 const out=prevFinish(win);
 if(a&&mode!=='lab'){
  const s=state();s.stats.battles++;s.stats.wins+=win?1:0;s.stats.losses+=win?0:1;s.stats.combo+=combo;s.stats.dodges+=dodges;
  s.stats.streak=win?s.stats.streak+1:0;s.stats.bestStreak=Math.max(s.stats.bestStreak,s.stats.streak);
  s.records.unshift({at:Date.now(),win:!!win,enemy:mvName(b.foe||{}),mode:b.label||'',combo,bonus});s.records=s.records.slice(0,40);
  if(bonus>0){save.coins+=bonus;s.stats.rewards+=bonus;persist();document.querySelector('.result')?.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend','<p class="v34Reward">✦ Bônus de desempenho: +'+bonus+' moedas</p>');}
 }
 return out;};
// 5, 9, 11: menu ficha, Pokédex, histórico.
render=function(){prevRender();if(!save.starter||battle?.active)return;
 const ver=$('v30VersionTag');if(ver)ver.textContent='V35.11 · SPRITES DS';

 parent.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',box);$('v34Stats').onclick=historyMenu;$('v34Quality').onclick=()=>{const q=state().settings.quality;state().settings.quality=q==='auto'?'low':q==='low'?'high':'auto';persist();render();const label=$('v34Quality');if(label)label.textContent='⚙️ Qualidade: '+state().settings.quality;};
};
function historyMenu(){const s=state().stats;const rows=state().records;

  return found?.key||fallback[i];
 });
};

const v342Catalog=v32Catalogue;
v32Catalogue=function(m){const base=v342Catalog(m);if(!m||!D[m.id])return base;
 const p=D[m.id],names=new Set([...(STATUS_BY_TYPE[p.type]||[]),...(STATUS_BY_TYPE[p.type2]||[]),...(STATUS_SPECIES[m.id]||[])]);
 const existing=new Set(base.map(x=>x.key));
 for(const name of names){const entry=STATUS_DEFS[name];if(!entry)continue;
  const [type,level,cost,cd]=entry,move={name,type,kind:'status',cat:'status',power:0,cost,cd,icon:'✧'};
  const key=v32MoveKey(move);if(existing.has(key))continue;
  base.push({key,move,level,tm:false,available:m.level>=level});existing.add(key);
 }
 return base;
};
// Estatísticas já existentes no motor V25 — status agora pode estar em qualquer posição 1–4.
const v342OldUse=BattleEngine.prototype.useMove;
BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){
 const b=battle,m=b?.active?current():null,s=m&&skillSet(m)?.[i];
 const cfg=s&&STATUS_DEFS[s.name];
 if(!cfg||!s||s.kind!=='status')return v342OldUse.call(this,i);
 if(!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||i>3||!b.active||b.paused||!this.v25?.stats||m.hp<=0)return;
 const now=performance.now();if((cooldowns[i]||0)>now||b.energy<s.cost)return;
 const action=cfg[4],delta=cfg[5];const self=this.v25.stats.player,enemy=this.v25.stats.enemy;
 const enemyTypes=[D[b.foe.id]?.type,D[b.foe.id]?.type2];
 if(action==='poison'&&enemyTypes.some(t=>['poison','steel'].includes(t))){log('Não afeta o adversário!');this.v25Popup(this.enemy.x,this.enemy.y-this.h*.27,'NÃO AFETA','#ffddd5');return;}
 if(action==='paralysis'&&enemyTypes.includes('ground')){log('Não afeta o adversário terrestre!');return;}
 if(action==='sleep'&&enemyTypes.includes('grass')&&/Powder|Spore/.test(s.name)){log('Pó não afeta Pokémon de Planta!');return;}
 b.energy-=s.cost;cooldowns[i]=now+Math.max(1000,s.cd);this.attackActs.player=this.time+.4;
 const buff=(st,field,amount)=>st[field]=Math.min(1.9,Math.max(.45,(st[field]||1)+amount));
 const cond=(name,duration)=>{if(!enemy.conditions.includes(name))enemy.conditions.push(name);this.v342CondTimers||={};this.v342CondTimers[name]=now+duration;};
 let target=this.enemy,label='',tint='#e4d2ff';
 switch(action){
 case 'atkdown':buff(enemy,'atk',-delta);label='ATK ↓';break;
 case 'defdown':buff(enemy,'def',-delta);label='DEF ↓';break;
 case 'speeddown':buff(enemy,'speed',-delta);label='VEL ↓';break;
 case 'accuracydown':buff(enemy,'accuracy',-delta);label='PRECISÃO ↓';break;
 case 'atkup':buff(self,'atk',delta);label='ATK ↑';target=this.player;break;
 case 'defup':buff(self,'def',delta);label='DEF ↑';target=this.player;break;
 case 'speedup':buff(self,'speed',delta);label='VEL ↑';target=this.player;break;
 case 'mindup':buff(self,'atk',delta);buff(self,'def',delta*.5);label='ATK/DEF ↑';target=this.player;break;
 case 'bulk':buff(self,'atk',delta);buff(self,'def',delta);label='ATK/DEF ↑';target=this.player;break;
 case 'dance':buff(self,'atk',delta);buff(self,'speed',delta);label='ATK/VEL ↑';target=this.player;break;
 case 'reflect':buff(self,'def',delta);this.player.guard=Math.max(this.player.guard,1.7);target=this.player;label='BARREIRA ↑';break;
 case 'protect':this.player.guard=Math.max(this.player.guard,2.0);this.player.inv=Math.max(this.player.inv,.48);target=this.player;label='PROTEGIDO';break;
 case 'cleanse':self.conditions.length=0;this.player.guard=Math.max(this.player.guard,1.4);target=this.player;label='PROTEÇÃO';break;
 case 'haze':for(const st of [self,enemy])st.atk=st.def=st.speed=st.accuracy=1;label='ATRIBUTOS RESETADOS';break;
 case 'paralysis':cond('paralysis',6500);label='PARALISADO';break;
 case 'poison':cond('poison',11500);label='ENVENENADO';break;
 case 'confusion':cond('confusion',6500);label='CONFUSO';break;
 case 'sleep':cond('sleep',2800);this.enemyAttackCD=Math.max(this.enemyAttackCD,2.8);label='DORMINDO';break;
 case 'burn':cond('burn',9500);label='QUEIMADO';break;
 case 'seed':cond('seed',10500);label='SEMENTES';break;
 case 'heal':{const heal=Math.max(1,Math.round(m.maxHp*delta));m.hp=Math.min(m.maxHp,m.hp+heal);target=this.player;label='PV +'+heal;break;}
 case 'evasion':this.player.inv=Math.max(this.player.inv,1.15);target=this.player;label='ESQUIVA ↑';break;
 case 'rain':b.v32Weather='rain';label='CHUVA';target=this.player;break;
 case 'sun':b.v32Weather='sun';label='SOL FORTE';target=this.player;break;
 case 'sand':b.v32Weather='sand';label='TEMPESTADE';target=this.player;break;
 default:b.energy+=s.cost;cooldowns[i]=0;return v342OldUse.call(this,i);
 }
 if(typeof this.v25Popup==='function')this.v25Popup(target.x,target.y-this.h*.32,label,tint);
 this.v342StatusFx||=[];this.v342StatusFx.push({name:s.name,x:target===this.player?'player':'enemy',start:now,end:now+620,color:TYPE_COLOR[s.type]||'#d6f3ff'});
 if(this.v342StatusFx.length>10)this.v342StatusFx.shift();
 log(D[m.id].name+' usou '+s.name+'! '+label);
 try{v25StatusChip(target===this.player?'ally':'enemy',label)}catch(_){}
};
const v342PrevUpdate=BattleEngine.prototype.update;
BattleEngine.prototype.update=function(dt,now){
 const res=v342PrevUpdate.call(this,dt,now);if(!battle?.active||!this.v25)return res;
 const stats=this.v25.stats.enemy,timers=this.v342CondTimers||{};
 for(const [condition,end] of Object.entries(timers))if(now>=end){stats.conditions=stats.conditions.filter(x=>x!==condition);delete timers[condition];}
 if(stats.conditions.includes('sleep'))this.enemyAttackCD=Math.max(this.enemyAttackCD,Math.min(.26,(timers.sleep-now)/1000));
 if(stats.conditions.includes('burn')||stats.conditions.includes('seed')){
  this.v342DamageTicker=(this.v342DamageTicker||0)+dt;
  if(this.v342DamageTicker>=2.0){this.v342DamageTicker=0;const foeMon=foe();if(foeMon){
   const burn=stats.conditions.includes('burn'),seed=stats.conditions.includes('seed');
   const amount=Math.max(1,Math.round(foeMon.maxHp*(burn?.025:.019)));
   foeMon.hp=Math.max(1,foeMon.hp-amount);
   if(seed&&current())current().hp=Math.min(current().maxHp,current().hp+amount);
   this.v25Popup(this.enemy.x,this.enemy.y-this.h*.22,(burn?'QUEIMADURA':'DRENADO')+' -'+amount,burn?'#ffb894':'#a6e8a1');
  }}
 }
 return res;
};
// RESET de efeitos ao trocar o inimigo, para que veneno/sono não passem a outro Pokémon.
const v342OldNewEnemy=BattleEngine.prototype.newEnemy;
BattleEngine.prototype.newEnemy=function(){
 this.v342CondTimers={};this.v342DamageTicker=0;
 const result=v342OldNewEnemy.call(this);
 if(this.v25?.stats?.enemy){this.v25.stats.enemy.conditions=[];this.v25.stats.enemy.ticker=0;}
 return result;
};
// Raios não são esferas: um caminho poligonal com ramificações no Canvas existente.
function v342Bolt(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,segments,seed,alpha=1,branches=true){
 const dx=x2-x1,dy=y2-y1,length=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,nx=-dy/length,ny=dx/length;
 if(length<3)return;
 const pts=[{x:x1,y:y1}];
 const wiggle=Math.min(24,Math.max(7,length*.12));
 for(let i=1;i<segments;i++){
  const t=i/segments,w=Math.sin(t*Math.PI)*wiggle;
  const r=Math.sin(seed*7.23+i*31.17)*Math.cos(seed*.71+i*17.23);
  pts.push({x:x1+dx*t+nx*r*w,y:y1+dy*t+ny*r*w});
 }pts.push({x:x2,y:y2});
 const trace=()=>{ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x1,y1);for(const p of pts.slice(1))ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.stroke();};
 ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.lineJoin='miter';ctx.lineCap='round';
 ctx.shadowColor='#79d8ff';ctx.shadowBlur=16;ctx.strokeStyle='#3e9fff';ctx.lineWidth=7;trace();
 ctx.shadowBlur=9;ctx.strokeStyle='#a8e6ff';ctx.lineWidth=3.5;trace();
 ctx.shadowBlur=2;ctx.strokeStyle='#ffffff';ctx.lineWidth=1.35;trace();
 if(branches){ctx.strokeStyle='#9ce8ff';ctx.shadowBlur=6;ctx.lineWidth=1.35;
  for(let i=2;i<pts.length-1;i+=Math.max(2,Math.floor(segments/4))){const p=pts[i];const side=i%2?1:-1,bl=length*.13;
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.lineTo(p.x+nx*bl*side+dx*.05,p.y+ny*bl*side+dy*.05);
   ctx.lineTo(p.x+nx*bl*1.5*side+dx*.08,p.y+ny*bl*1.5*side+dy*.08);ctx.stroke();
  }
 }
 ctx.restore();
}
const v342Projectile=BattleEngine.prototype.drawProjectile;
BattleEngine.prototype.drawProjectile=function(p){
 if(p.type!=='electric')return v342Projectile.call(this,p);
 // Substitui integralmente o círculo elétrico legado pelo raio ramificado.
 const tail=p.tail?.[0]||{x:p.x-p.vx*.045,y:p.y-p.vy*.045};
 v342Bolt(this.ctx,tail.x,tail.y,p.x,p.y,8,this.time*10+(p.x+p.y)*.019,.95,true);
};
const v342Draw=BattleEngine.prototype.draw;
BattleEngine.prototype.draw=function(now){
 v342Draw.call(this,now);if(!battle?.active)return;
 const ctx=this.ctx,b=battle;const low=this.fps&&this.fps<28;
 ctx.save();
 for(const a of this.v342Lightning||[]){const elapsed=now-a.start;if(elapsed>=a.duration)continue;
  const fade=Math.max(0,1-elapsed/a.duration);
  const start=a.from==='enemy'?this.enemy:this.player,target=a.from==='enemy'?this.player:this.enemy;
  v342Bolt(ctx,start.x,start.y-this.h*.13,target.x,target.y-this.h*.12,low?8:12,now*.025+a.seed,fade*.89,!low);
 }
 // Projéteis teleguiados elétricos desenhados como arcos no espaço, não como bolas.
 for(const p of this.v30Shots||[]){if(p.type!=='electric')continue;
  const previous=p.trail?.[0]||{x:p.x-p.vx*.04,y:p.y-p.vy*.04};
  v342Bolt(ctx,previous.x,previous.y,p.x,p.y,7,now*.02+p.x*.01,.95,!low);
 }
 for(const a of this.v342StatusFx||[]){const f=(a.end-now)/(a.end-a.start);if(f<=0)continue;
  const target=a.x==='player'?this.player:this.enemy,rad=this.h*.065*(1.3-f);
  ctx.globalAlpha=.75*f;ctx.strokeStyle=a.color;ctx.shadowColor=a.color;ctx.shadowBlur=6;
  ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(target.x,target.y-this.h*.12,rad,rad*.62,-.2,Math.PI*.18,Math.PI*1.82);ctx.stroke();
  for(let k=0;k<(low?4:9);k++){
   const angle=(k/(low?4:9))*Math.PI*2+now*.002;
   const px=target.x+Math.cos(angle)*rad,py=target.y-this.h*.12+Math.sin(angle)*rad*.56;
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(px-2,py-2);ctx.lineTo(px+2,py+2);ctx.moveTo(px+2,py-2);ctx.lineTo(px-2,py+2);ctx.stroke();
  }
 }
 ctx.restore();
 this.v342Lightning=(this.v342Lightning||[]).filter(x=>now-x.start<x.duration);
 this.v342StatusFx=(this.v342StatusFx||[]).filter(x=>now<x.end);
};
const v342VisualUse=BattleEngine.prototype.useMove;
BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){
 const b=battle,m=b?.active?current():null,s=m?skillSet(m)[i]:null,now=performance.now(),before=cooldowns[i];
 const result=v342VisualUse.call(this,i);
 if(s?.type==='electric'&&s.power>0&&b===battle&&b?.active&&cooldowns[i]>now&&cooldowns[i]!==before){
  this.v342Lightning||=[];
  this.v342Lightning.push({from:'player',start:now,duration:s.kind==='beam'?360:225,seed:Math.random()*80});
  if(this.v342Lightning.length>6)this.v342Lightning.shift();
 }
 return result;
};
const v342VisualHit=BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget;
BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget=function(side,type,power){
 const result=v342VisualHit.call(this,side,type,power);
 if(type==='electric'&&battle?.active){this.v342Lightning||=[];this.v342Lightning.push({from:side==='enemy'?'player':'enemy',start:performance.now(),duration:160,seed:Math.random()*100});}
 return result;
};

.v342LeaderRoster{flex:1 0 100%;font-size:10px;line-height:1.5;color:#dbf5fe;padding:6px 9px;border-left:2px solid #75bff4;background:#143047aa;border-radius:6px;margin-top:5px;max-width:100%}
.battleHeading{flex-wrap:wrap}
.skillV8.kind-status .skillAura{opacity:.2}
.skillV8.kind-status{border-color:#69889f!important}
.skillV8.kind-status .skillIcon{filter:none}
@media(max-width:500px){.v342LeaderRoster{font-size:9px}.skillV8.kind-status .skillIcon{font-size:15px}}
`;document.head.append(v342Css);
window.PokemonTorreV342Diagnostics=()=>({version:V342,leaders:KANTO_LEADERS_FRLG.map((x,i)=>({name:GYMS[i].leader,team:teamOfTrainer(i,v29TrainerCount(i)).map(m=>[m.id,m.level])})),statuses:Object.keys(STATUS_DEFS).length,exampleStatusMoves:v32Catalogue(mon(25,55)).filter(x=>x.move.cat==='status').map(x=>x.move.name),electricDraw:'lightning-path-canvas',singleplayer:true});
