/* POKEMON TORRE V34.3 — efeitos elaborados para todos os tipos */
(function(){
'use strict';
const V343='34.3';
const ARENA_BG = '#172839';
const TYPE_VFX={
 electric:{cast:'#7dd6ff',mid:'#3fa5ff',core:'#ffffff',style:'lightning'},
 fire:{cast:'#ffb347',mid:'#ff6b2e',core:'#fff1c1',style:'flame'},
 water:{cast:'#8fe8ff',mid:'#36b6ff',core:'#ffffff',style:'water'},
 ice:{cast:'#d6f6ff',mid:'#7fd8ff',core:'#ffffff',style:'ice'},
 grass:{cast:'#b5ff8d',mid:'#53c96a',core:'#efffde',style:'grass'},
 poison:{cast:'#d8a1ff',mid:'#8f4fe0',core:'#fff2ff',style:'poison'},
 psychic:{cast:'#ffb1fb',mid:'#e96dff',core:'#ffffff',style:'psychic'},
 ghost:{cast:'#caa8ff',mid:'#7d65ff',core:'#f8f1ff',style:'ghost'},
 dark:{cast:'#9ba5cf',mid:'#4b557d',core:'#ffffff',style:'dark'},
 ground:{cast:'#d6b084',mid:'#8c5a31',core:'#fff1dc',style:'earth'},
 rock:{cast:'#e6c495',mid:'#9c7445',core:'#fff5e8',style:'rock'},
 bug:{cast:'#d4ff75',mid:'#8dcb2f',core:'#fffeda',style:'bug'},
 flying:{cast:'#e8fbff',mid:'#b0e0ff',core:'#ffffff',style:'wind'},
 dragon:{cast:'#7fe1ff',mid:'#2d8bff',core:'#f6d0ff',style:'dragon'},
 steel:{cast:'#edf5ff',mid:'#93a9bd',core:'#ffffff',style:'steel'},
 fairy:{cast:'#ffd6f0',mid:'#ff8fdb',core:'#ffffff',style:'fairy'},
 fighting:{cast:'#ffd59a',mid:'#ff8f4f',core:'#fff7e8',style:'impact'},
 normal:{cast:'#f7eedf',mid:'#d9cdb8',core:'#ffffff',style:'impact'}
};
function v343Cfg(type){return TYPE_VFX[type]||TYPE_VFX.normal;}
function lerp(a,b,t){return a+(b-a)*t;}
function clamp(x,a,b){return Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));}
function randSeed(seed){const x=Math.sin(seed*12.9898)*43758.5453;return x-Math.floor(x);}
function circle(ctx,x,y,r,color,alpha=1,blur=0){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.shadowColor=color;ctx.shadowBlur=blur;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}
function line(ctx,pts,w,color,alpha=1,blur=0){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.lineWidth=w;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.shadowColor=color;ctx.shadowBlur=blur;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pts[0].x,pts[0].y);for(const p of pts.slice(1))ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
function bolt(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha=1,branch=4){const dx=x2-x1,dy=y2-y1,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,nx=-dy/len,ny=dx/len;const pts=[{x:x1,y:y1}];const seg=10,jit=Math.min(22,Math.max(8,len*.12));for(let i=1;i<seg;i++){const t=i/seg;const off=(randSeed(seed+i*4.1)*2-1)*jit*Math.sin(Math.PI*t);pts.push({x:x1+dx*t+nx*off,y:y1+dy*t+ny*off});}pts.push({x:x2,y:y2});line(ctx,pts,7,'#4ea4ff',alpha*.45,16);line(ctx,pts,3,'#a5ebff',alpha*.9,9);line(ctx,pts,1.2,'#ffffff',alpha,0);for(let i=2;i<pts.length-1;i+=Math.max(2,Math.floor(seg/branch))){const p=pts[i];const side=i%2?1:-1;const bl=len*.16;const q={x:p.x+nx*bl*side+dx*.06,y:p.y+ny*bl*side+dy*.04};const r={x:p.x+nx*bl*1.45*side+dx*.1,y:p.y+ny*bl*1.45*side+dy*.08};line(ctx,[p,q,r],1.2,'#caf7ff',alpha*.75,5);}}
function ribbon(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha,c1,c2,width=7,wave=12){const dx=x2-x1,dy=y2-y1,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,nx=-dy/len,ny=dx/len;const pts=[];for(let i=0;i<=14;i++){const t=i/14;const off=Math.sin(seed+i*.9)*wave*Math.sin(Math.PI*t);pts.push({x:x1+dx*t+nx*off,y:y1+dy*t+ny*off});}line(ctx,pts,width,c1,alpha*.45,14);line(ctx,pts,width*.56,c2,alpha*.95,7);line(ctx,pts,width*.2,'#ffffff',alpha*.95,0);}
function fanFlame(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){const dx=x2-x1,dy=y2-y1,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx),spread=.48;for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const t=i/6-0.5;const a=ang+t*spread;const l=len*(.55+randSeed(seed+i)*.45);const ex=x1+Math.cos(a)*l,ey=y1+Math.sin(a)*l;ribbon(ctx,x1,y1,ex,ey,seed+i*2.1,alpha*.8,'#ff7f2f','#ffd27a',5,4);circle(ctx,lerp(x1,ex,.7),lerp(y1,ey,.7),2.4,'#ffb347',alpha*.75,5);}for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const t=randSeed(seed+i*3.3);circle(ctx,lerp(x1,x2,t),lerp(y1,y2,t)-t*6,1.8+randSeed(seed+i)*1.8,'#ff944d',alpha*(1-t),4);}}
function waterJet(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){ribbon(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha,'#3cbcff','#b7f0ff',8,8);for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const t=i/9;const rr=2.3*(1-t)+.8;circle(ctx,lerp(x1,x2,t),lerp(y1,y2,t)+(Math.sin(seed+t*14)*4),rr,'#d9fbff',alpha*(1-t),4);} }
function iceBeam(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){ribbon(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha,'#92e3ff','#ffffff',6,4);for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const t=i/7;const x=lerp(x1,x2,t),y=lerp(y1,y2,t);line(ctx,[{x:x-3,y:y},{x:x+3,y:y},{x:x,y:y-3},{x:x,y:y+3}],1,'#eaffff',alpha*.7,0);}}
function leafTrail(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const t=i/7;const x=lerp(x1,x2,t),y=lerp(y1,y2,t)+Math.sin(seed+t*13)*8;const a=seed+t*8;ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(a);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*(1-t*.6);ctx.fillStyle=i%2?'#53c96a':'#b5ff8d';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-6,0);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0,-4,6,0);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0,4,-6,0);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}line(ctx,[{x:x1,y:y1},{x:x2,y:y2}],1.4,'#93df69',alpha*.35,4);}
function poisonCloud(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const t=randSeed(seed+i*5.1);const x=lerp(x1,x2,t)+(randSeed(seed+i*7)-.5)*12;const y=lerp(y1,y2,t)+(randSeed(seed+i*9)-.5)*10;circle(ctx,x,y,4+randSeed(seed+i*11)*7,i%2?'#9d52df':'#d89cff',alpha*(.7-t*.4),10);} }
function psychicWave(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const t=(seed*.5+i*.22)%1;const x=lerp(x1,x2,t),y=lerp(y1,y2,t);ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(seed+i);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*(1-t);ctx.strokeStyle=i%2?'#ff9bf7':'#ffffff';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.shadowColor='#ff8ef9';ctx.shadowBlur=8;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,10+i*4,4+i*2,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}ribbon(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha*.6,'#e96dff','#ffd9ff',4,14);}
function earthSpike(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){const dx=x2-x1,dy=y2-y1;for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const t=i/5;const x=lerp(x1,x2,t),y=lerp(y1,y2,t);const h=10+randSeed(seed+i)*10;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*(.4+.6*(1-t));ctx.fillStyle=i%2?'#8c5a31':'#d5aa74';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-5,y+6);ctx.lineTo(x,y-h);ctx.lineTo(x+6,y+5);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();} }
function rockShards(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const t=i/7;const x=lerp(x1,x2,t)+(randSeed(seed+i)-.5)*8,y=lerp(y1,y2,t)+(randSeed(seed+i*2)-.5)*6;ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(seed+i);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*(1-t*.5);ctx.fillStyle=i%2?'#c99b66':'#8a673f';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-5,-2);ctx.lineTo(-1,-6);ctx.lineTo(5,-1);ctx.lineTo(4,5);ctx.lineTo(-3,4);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}}
function windSlash(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const off=(i-1)*8;ribbon(ctx,x1,y1+off,x2,y2+off,seed+i,alpha*.72,'#ccefff','#ffffff',3,6);} }
function ghostTrail(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){ribbon(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha,'#6f61ff','#d4b9ff',5,18);for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const t=i/5;circle(ctx,lerp(x1,x2,t),lerp(y1,y2,t)-Math.sin(seed+t*9)*5,4.2*(1-t)+1.6,'#a17dff',alpha*(1-t),8);} }
function dragonSpiral(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const t=i/9;const a=seed+t*12;const rad=10*(1-t);circle(ctx,lerp(x1,x2,t)+Math.cos(a)*rad,lerp(y1,y2,t)+Math.sin(a)*rad*.6,2.3,'#6dd9ff',alpha*(1-t),5);circle(ctx,lerp(x1,x2,t)-Math.cos(a)*rad,lerp(y1,y2,t)-Math.sin(a)*rad*.6,1.9,'#f6b2ff',alpha*(1-t),4);} ribbon(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha*.45,'#2d8bff','#e9d0ff',4,4);}
function steelSlash(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const t=i/2;line(ctx,[{x:lerp(x1,x2,t)-10,y:lerp(y1,y2,t)-8},{x:lerp(x1,x2,t)+10,y:lerp(y1,y2,t)+8}],2.2,'#eef7ff',alpha*.9,4);line(ctx,[{x:lerp(x1,x2,t)-6,y:lerp(y1,y2,t)+8},{x:lerp(x1,x2,t)+12,y:lerp(y1,y2,t)-8}],1.2,'#9baec0',alpha*.8,2);} }
function impactBurst(ctx,x,y,seed,alpha,c1,c2){for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const a=(Math.PI*2*i)/10+seed;const r=8+randSeed(seed+i)*12;line(ctx,[{x,y},{x:x+Math.cos(a)*r,y:y+Math.sin(a)*r}],2,c1,alpha*.85,3);}circle(ctx,x,y,7,c2,alpha*.55,10);}
function fairyBurst(ctx,x,y,seed,alpha){for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const a=(Math.PI*2*i)/7+seed;const px=x+Math.cos(a)*12,py=y+Math.sin(a)*12;ctx.save();ctx.translate(px,py);ctx.rotate(a);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.fillStyle=i%2?'#ff8fdb':'#ffffff';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,-4);ctx.lineTo(1.5,-1);ctx.lineTo(5,0);ctx.lineTo(1.5,1);ctx.lineTo(0,4);ctx.lineTo(-1.5,1);ctx.lineTo(-5,0);ctx.lineTo(-1.5,-1);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}circle(ctx,x,y,8,'#ffd6f0',alpha*.5,9);}
function drawTravel(ctx,type,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha){const s=v343Cfg(type).style;switch(s){case'lightning':return bolt(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha,4);case'flame':return fanFlame(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);case'water':return waterJet(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);case'ice':return iceBeam(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);case'grass':return leafTrail(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);case'poison':return poisonCloud(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);case'psychic':return psychicWave(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);case'earth':return earthSpike(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);case'rock':return rockShards(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);case'wind':return windSlash(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);case'ghost':case'dark':return ghostTrail(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);case'dragon':return dragonSpiral(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);case'steel':return steelSlash(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,alpha);default:return impactBurst(ctx,(x1+x2)/2,(y1+y2)/2,seed,alpha,v343Cfg(type).mid,v343Cfg(type).core);} }
function drawImpact(ctx,type,x,y,seed,alpha){const cfg=v343Cfg(type);switch(cfg.style){case'lightning':bolt(ctx,x-18,y-8,x+18,y+8,seed,alpha,3);impactBurst(ctx,x,y,seed,alpha,'#9ce7ff','#ffffff');break;case'flame':impactBurst(ctx,x,y,seed,alpha,'#ff7a30','#ffe199');for(let i=0;i<9;i++)circle(ctx,x+(randSeed(seed+i)-.5)*20,y+(randSeed(seed+i*2)-.5)*14,2+randSeed(seed+i*3)*3,'#ff9a3a',alpha*.8,6);break;case'water':for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const a=(Math.PI*2*i)/12;line(ctx,[{x,y},{x:x+Math.cos(a)*(8+i%3*3),y:y+Math.sin(a)*(8+i%3*3)}],2,'#8fe8ff',alpha*.8,4);}circle(ctx,x,y,10,'#dffcff',alpha*.45,8);break;case'ice':impactBurst(ctx,x,y,seed,alpha,'#9ce9ff','#ffffff');for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const a=(Math.PI*2*i)/8;line(ctx,[{x,y},{x:x+Math.cos(a)*12,y:y+Math.sin(a)*12}],1.4,'#ffffff',alpha*.95,0);}break;case'grass':for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const a=(Math.PI*2*i)/8+seed;ctx.save();ctx.translate(x+Math.cos(a)*8,y+Math.sin(a)*8);ctx.rotate(a);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.fillStyle=i%2?'#89da65':'#d9ff9c';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-5,0);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0,-3,5,0);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0,3,-5,0);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}break;case'poison':poisonCloud(ctx,x-10,y,x+10,y,seed,alpha);break;case'psychic':for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(seed+i*.5);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*(1-i*.18);ctx.strokeStyle=i%2?'#ff9bf7':'#ffffff';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.shadowColor='#ff8ef9';ctx.shadowBlur=8;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,8+i*6,4+i*2,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}break;case'earth':case'rock':earthSpike(ctx,x-14,y+6,x+14,y+6,seed,alpha);rockShards(ctx,x-10,y,x+10,y,seed,alpha);break;case'wind':impactBurst(ctx,x,y,seed,alpha,'#dff7ff','#ffffff');break;case'ghost':case'dark':ghostTrail(ctx,x-12,y,x+12,y,seed,alpha);break;case'dragon':dragonSpiral(ctx,x-12,y,x+12,y,seed,alpha);break;case'steel':steelSlash(ctx,x-16,y-4,x+16,y+4,seed,alpha);impactBurst(ctx,x,y,seed,alpha,'#c7d3df','#ffffff');break;case'fairy':fairyBurst(ctx,x,y,seed,alpha);break;default:impactBurst(ctx,x,y,seed,alpha,cfg.mid,cfg.core);} }
const oldUse=BattleEngine.prototype.useMove;
BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){const b=battle,m=b?.active?current():null,s=m?skillSet(m)?.[i]:null;const now=performance.now();const before=(cooldowns||[])[i]||0;const res=oldUse.call(this,i);if(s&&!this.v343SuppressGeneric&&b===battle&&b?.active&&(cooldowns||[])[i]>now&&(cooldowns||[])[i]!==before){this.v343Fx||(this.v343Fx=[]);this.v343Fx.push({kind:'cast',type:s.type||'normal',start:now,end:now+Math.max(240,s.kind==='beam'?420:300),seed:Math.random()*1000,from:'player'});if(this.v343Fx.length>40)this.v343Fx.shift();}return res;};
const oldHit=BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget;
BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget=function(side,type,power){const res=oldHit.call(this,side,type,power);if(battle?.active&&type&&!this.v343SuppressGeneric){this.v343Fx||(this.v343Fx=[]);this.v343Fx.push({kind:'impact',type:type||'normal',start:performance.now(),end:performance.now()+360,seed:Math.random()*1000,from:side});if(this.v343Fx.length>50)this.v343Fx.shift();}return res;};
const oldProj=BattleEngine.prototype.drawProjectile;
BattleEngine.prototype.drawProjectile=function(p){const type=p?.type||'normal';if(['electric','fire','water','ice','grass','poison','psychic','ground','rock','ghost','dark','flying','dragon','steel','fairy'].includes(type)){const tail=p.tail?.[0]||{x:p.x-(p.vx||0)*.045,y:p.y-(p.vy||0)*.045};const ctx=this.ctx;ctx.save();circle(ctx,p.x,p.y,Math.max(5,(p.r||7)*1.55),ARENA_BG,1,0);drawTravel(ctx,type,tail.x,tail.y,p.x,p.y,performance.now()*.02+p.x*.01,.95);ctx.restore();return;}return oldProj.call(this,p);};
const oldDraw=BattleEngine.prototype.draw;
BattleEngine.prototype.draw=function(now){oldDraw.call(this,now);if(!battle?.active||!this.ctx)return;const ctx=this.ctx;const low=this.fps&&this.fps<27;const fx=(this.v343Fx||[]);ctx.save();for(const e of fx){if(now>e.end)continue;const a=clamp((e.end-now)/(e.end-e.start),0,1);const source=e.from==='enemy'?this.enemy:this.player;const target=e.from==='enemy'?this.player:this.enemy;if(e.kind==='cast'){drawTravel(ctx,e.type,source.x,source.y-this.h*.12,target.x,target.y-this.h*.12,e.seed+now*.003,low?a*.65:a);}else if(e.kind==='impact'){drawImpact(ctx,e.type,target.x,target.y-this.h*.1,e.seed+now*.003,low?a*.75:a);} }
 ctx.restore();this.v343Fx=fx.filter(e=>now<e.end);};
const style=document.createElement('style');style.id='v343_css';style.textContent=`
.skillBtn[data-type="electric"], .skillV8[data-type="electric"]{box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #3aa5ff44,inset 0 0 18px #7dd6ff22}
.skillBtn[data-type="fire"], .skillV8[data-type="fire"]{box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #ff8a3d44,inset 0 0 18px #ffb34722}
.skillBtn[data-type="water"], .skillV8[data-type="water"]{box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #4bbdff44,inset 0 0 18px #9deeff22}
.skillBtn[data-type="grass"], .skillV8[data-type="grass"]{box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #54cb6b44,inset 0 0 18px #b6ff8d22}
.skillBtn[data-type="psychic"], .skillV8[data-type="psychic"]{box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #e96dff44,inset 0 0 18px #ffb1fb22}
.skillBtn[data-type="poison"], .skillV8[data-type="poison"]{box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #8f4fe044,inset 0 0 18px #d8a1ff22}
`;document.head.appendChild(style);
window.PokemonTorreV343Diagnostics=()=>({version:V343,types:Object.keys(TYPE_VFX),fxCount:(engine&&engine.v343Fx?engine.v343Fx.length:0),styles:Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(TYPE_VFX).map(([k,v])=>[k,v.style]))});
})();
/* FIM POKEMON TORRE V34.3 */

/* V34.3 Signature moves: frame-driven trails and impacts for 11 named attacks */
(function(){
'use strict';
const SIGNATURES={
 'thunderbolt':'thunderbolt','raio':'thunderbolt','choque do trovão':'thunderbolt',
 'thunder':'thunder','trovão':'thunder',
 'flamethrower':'flamethrower','lança-chamas':'flamethrower',
 'fire blast':'fireblast','explosão de fogo':'fireblast',
 'surf':'surf','surfar':'surf',
 'hydro pump':'hydropump','hidro bomba':'hydropump',
 'ice beam':'icebeam','raio de gelo':'icebeam',
 'psychic':'psychic','psíquico':'psychic',
 'shadow ball':'shadowball','bola sombria':'shadowball',
 'earthquake':'earthquake','terremoto':'earthquake',
 'rock slide':'rockslide','avalanche de pedras':'rockslide'
};
function signature(name){return SIGNATURES[String(name||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase()] || SIGNATURES[String(name||'').toLowerCase()] || null;}
function q01(n){return Math.min(1,Math.max(0,n));}
function vfxQuality(eng){const quality=save?.v34?.settings?.quality;const reduce=window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')?.matches;return reduce||quality==='baixo'||(eng.fps>0&&eng.fps<32)?'low':quality==='alto'&&eng.fps>=48?'high':'medium';}
function rand(n){const x=Math.sin(n*79.7+14.13)*42319.19;return x-Math.floor(x);}
function mix(a,b,t){return a+(b-a)*t;}
function stroke(ctx,points,color,width,opacity=1,glow=0){ctx.save();ctx.lineWidth=width;ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.globalAlpha=q01(opacity);ctx.shadowColor=color;ctx.shadowBlur=glow;ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.beginPath();points.forEach((p,i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(p.x,p.y):ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y));ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
function shard(ctx,x,y,angle,size,color,alpha=1){ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(angle);ctx.globalAlpha=q01(alpha);ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-size*.66,size*.45);ctx.lineTo(-size*.1,-size);ctx.lineTo(size*.65,-size*.3);ctx.lineTo(size*.35,size*.66);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}
function ring(ctx,x,y,rx,ry,color,alpha=1,rotation=0){ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(rotation);ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.globalAlpha=q01(alpha);ctx.shadowColor=color;ctx.shadowBlur=5;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,rx,ry,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
function flame(ctx,ox,oy,tx,ty,seed,alpha,quality){const x=tx-ox,y=ty-oy,len=Math.hypot(x,y)||1,perp={x:-y/len,y:x/len};const n=quality==='low'?3:quality==='high'?9:6;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const w=(i-(n-1)/2)/n,l=(.64+rand(seed+i*3)*.31),px=ox+x*l+perp.x*w*len*.18,py=oy+y*l+perp.y*w*len*.18;const pts=[{x:ox,y:oy},{x:ox+x*.22+perp.x*w*len*.04,y:oy+y*.22+perp.y*w*len*.04},{x:ox+x*.47+perp.x*w*len*.1,y:oy+y*.47+perp.y*w*len*.1},{x:px,y:py}];stroke(ctx,pts,i%2?'#ff712c':'#ffa43d',quality==='low'?4.5:7,alpha*.7,quality==='low'?0:9);stroke(ctx,pts,'#ffe8ad',quality==='low'?1.6:2,alpha*.9,0);} }
function lightning(ctx,x,y,tx,ty,seed,alpha,branches,low=false){const dx=tx-x,dy=ty-y,l=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,nx=-dy/l,ny=dx/l;const pts=[{x,y}],N=low?7:12;for(let i=1;i<N;i++){const t=i/N,off=(rand(seed+i*2.11)-.5)*Math.min(37,l*.23)*Math.sin(Math.PI*t);pts.push({x:x+dx*t+nx*off,y:y+dy*t+ny*off});}pts.push({x:tx,y:ty});stroke(ctx,pts,'#358eff',low?4:7,alpha*.55,low?0:12);stroke(ctx,pts,'#b9f2ff',low?2:3.3,alpha,low?0:5);stroke(ctx,pts,'#ffffff',.85,alpha,0);for(let i=2;i<N-1;i+=low?4:2){const p=pts[i],dir=i%2?1:-1,t=low?11:21;stroke(ctx,[p,{x:p.x+nx*t*dir+dx*.055,y:p.y+ny*t*dir+dy*.055},{x:p.x+nx*t*1.4*dir+dx*.09,y:p.y+ny*t*1.4*dir+dy*.09}],'#c9f8ff',1.2,alpha*.85,0);} }
function spray(ctx,x,y,tx,ty,seed,alpha,quality){const low=quality==='low',waves=low?2:5;for(let j=0;j<waves;j++){const pts=[];for(let i=0;i<=14;i++){const t=i/14,off=Math.sin(t*13+seed+j)*((j%2?1:-1)*4+j*2)*Math.sin(Math.PI*t);pts.push({x:mix(x,tx,t),y:mix(y,ty,t)+off});}stroke(ctx,pts,j%2?'#2eacff':'#72e0ff',low?4:6,alpha*.65,low?0:8);stroke(ctx,pts,'#e8ffff',1.4,alpha,0);} }
function signatureVfx(ctx,name,phase,ax,ay,bx,by,progress,opacity,seed,quality){
 const low=quality==='low',n=low?5:quality==='high'?16:10,p=q01(progress),a=q01(opacity);if(a<.025)return;
 if(phase==='impact'){
  switch(name){
   case 'thunderbolt':case 'thunder':for(let i=0;i<(low?3:7);i++){const ang=2*Math.PI*i/(low?3:7);lightning(ctx,bx,by,bx+Math.cos(ang)*(17+20*p),by+Math.sin(ang)*(17+20*p),seed+i,a*(1-p),2,low);}break;
   case 'flamethrower':case 'fireblast':for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const ang=i*2.399+seed,rr=(8+rand(i+seed)*18)*p;shard(ctx,bx+Math.cos(ang)*rr,by+Math.sin(ang)*rr,ang,4+6*(1-p),i%2?'#ff7026':'#ffcd73',a*(1-p));}break;
   case 'surf':case 'hydropump':for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const ang=i*2.4+seed,r=(12+rand(seed+i)*23)*p;ring(ctx,bx+Math.cos(ang)*r,by+Math.sin(ang)*r,2.8,4.2,'#c0f8ff',a*(1-p),ang);}ring(ctx,bx,by,10+16*p,5+8*p,'#90ddff',a*(1-p));break;
   case 'icebeam':for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const ang=i*2.399+seed,r=(7+24*p);shard(ctx,bx+Math.cos(ang)*r,by+Math.sin(ang)*r,ang,5+8*p,i%2?'#c2f8ff':'#ffffff',a*(1-p));}break;
   case 'psychic':for(let i=0;i<(low?2:4);i++)ring(ctx,bx,by,8+18*p+i*6,3+8*p+i*2,i%2?'#f8adff':'#a985ff',a*(1-p),seed+i*.43);break;
   case 'shadowball':for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const ang=i*2.399+seed;shard(ctx,bx+Math.cos(ang)*(8+24*p),by+Math.sin(ang)*(8+24*p),ang,5,'#8e65c7',a*(1-p));}break;
   case 'earthquake':for(let i=0;i<(low?3:7);i++){const dir=i*2.4+seed,d=26*p;stroke(ctx,[{x:bx,y:by},{x:bx+Math.cos(dir)*d*.55,y:by+Math.sin(dir)*d*.35},{x:bx+Math.cos(dir+(.4*(i%2?1:-1)))*d,y:by+Math.sin(dir)*d*.55}],'#d9ad75',low?1.5:2.3,a*(1-p),0);}break;
   case 'rockslide':for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x=bx+(rand(seed+i)-.5)*38,y=by-18+25*p+rand(seed+i*3)*13;shard(ctx,x,y,seed+i,4+rand(i+seed)*5,i%2?'#ae8451':'#e3bd82',a*(1-p));}break;
  }
  return;
 }
 switch(name){
  case 'thunderbolt':{for(let j=0;j<(low?1:2);j++)lightning(ctx,ax,ay,bx,by,seed+j*5+Math.floor(p*12),a*.9,4,low);break;}
  case 'thunder':{const top=by-120-(low?0:45);lightning(ctx,bx+(rand(seed)-.5)*9,top,bx,by,seed+Math.floor(p*11),a,6,low);if(!low)lightning(ctx,ax,ay,bx,by,seed+5,a*.5,3,false);break;}
  case 'flamethrower':{flame(ctx,ax,ay,mix(ax,bx,p),mix(ay,by,p),seed+p*4,a,quality);break;}
  case 'fireblast':{flame(ctx,ax,ay,bx,by,seed+p*4,a,quality);const rad=(low?23:37)*(.4+.6*p);for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const angle=(i*4*Math.PI/5)+seed*.02;stroke(ctx,[{x:bx,y:by},{x:bx+Math.cos(angle)*rad,y:by+Math.sin(angle)*rad}],'#ffb753',low?2:4,a,low?0:8);}break;}
  case 'surf':{const w=low?68:108,x=bx-45*p,y=by+19-25*p;for(let k=0;k<(low?2:4);k++){const pts=[];for(let i=0;i<=16;i++){const t=i/16;pts.push({x:x-w/2+t*w,y:y+Math.sin(t*7.2-p*4.5+k*.9)*(4+k*2)});}stroke(ctx,pts,k%2?'#b8faff':'#37b7ff',low?3.5:5,a*(1-p*.4),low?0:6);}break;}
  case 'hydropump':{spray(ctx,ax,ay,bx,by,seed+p*4,a,quality);if(!low){const x=mix(ax,bx,p),y=mix(ay,by,p);for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const ang=i*2.4+seed;ring(ctx,x+Math.cos(ang)*11,y+Math.sin(ang)*7,2,3,'#f2ffff',a*.75,ang);}}break;}
  case 'icebeam':{stroke(ctx,[{x:ax,y:ay},{x:bx,y:by}],'#91dbfb',low?4:8,a*.7,low?0:10);stroke(ctx,[{x:ax,y:ay},{x:bx,y:by}],'#f8ffff',low?1.7:3,a,0);for(let i=0;i<(low?3:9);i++){const t=(i+(p*3)%1)/(low?3:9),x=mix(ax,bx,t),y=mix(ay,by,t);shard(ctx,x,y+(i%2?7:-7),seed+i,3.5,'#bcf5ff',a);}break;}
  case 'psychic':{const x=mix(ax,bx,p),y=mix(ay,by,p);for(let i=0;i<(low?2:5);i++)ring(ctx,x,y,7+i*5,4+i*2,i%2?'#ffd2ff':'#df7cff',a*(1-i*.13),seed+p*4+i*.4);break;}
  case 'shadowball':{const x=mix(ax,bx,p),y=mix(ay,by,p);for(let i=0;i<(low?4:12);i++){const ang=i*2.399+seed+p*6,r=5+(i%4)*2.5;shard(ctx,x+Math.cos(ang)*r,y+Math.sin(ang)*r,ang,3+(i%3),'#b899fc',a*.75);}ring(ctx,x,y,8,4,'#7365cf',a*.8,seed+p*6);break;}
  case 'earthquake':{const dx=bx-ax,dy=by-ay;for(let j=0;j<(low?1:3);j++){const pts=[{x:ax,y:ay+18}];for(let i=1;i<=9;i++){const t=i/9;pts.push({x:ax+dx*t,y:ay+18+dy*t+(rand(seed+i+j*11)-.5)*12});}stroke(ctx,pts,j===0?'#6e442b':'#dfb07a',j===0?low?4:6:2,a*(1-p*.3),low?0:4);}break;}
  case 'rockslide':{for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x=bx+(rand(seed+i)-.5)*66,y=by-90+95*((p+i/n)%1);shard(ctx,x,y,seed+i+p*3,5+rand(i+seed*2)*6,i%2?'#a88453':'#d4b27c',a);}break;}
 }
}
const baseUse=BattleEngine.prototype.useMove;
BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){const b=battle,mon=b?.active?current():null,move=mon?skillSet(mon)?.[i]:null,kind=signature(move?.name);const now=performance.now();const before=(cooldowns||[])[i]||0;const previousProjectiles=this.projectiles?.length||0;const previousSpecial=this.v30Shots?.length||0;
 if(kind&&move?.power>0)this.v343Pending={kind,type:move.type,at:now};
 this.v343SuppressGeneric=kind;let ret;try{ret=baseUse.call(this,i);}finally{this.v343SuppressGeneric=null;}
 if(!kind||!b?.active||b!==battle||(cooldowns||[])[i]<=now||(cooldowns||[])[i]===before)return ret;
 const quality=vfxQuality(this);this.v343Named||(this.v343Named=[]);
 this.v343Named.push({kind,phase:'travel',from:'player',start:now,duration:quality==='low'?290:420,seed:Math.random()*200});this.v343Named=this.v343Named.slice(-8);
 if(this.projectiles)for(let k=previousProjectiles;k<this.projectiles.length;k++)this.projectiles[k].v343Signature=kind;
 if(this.v30Shots)for(let k=previousSpecial;k<this.v30Shots.length;k++)this.v30Shots[k].v343Signature=kind;
 this.v343Pending={kind,type:move.type,at:now};return ret;
};
const baseHit=BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget;
BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget=function(side,type,power){const pending=this.v343Pending;const matched=pending&&side==='enemy'&&pending.type===type&&performance.now()-pending.at<2400;const ret=baseHit.call(this,side,type,power);if(battle?.active&&matched){this.v343Named||(this.v343Named=[]);this.v343Named.push({kind:pending.kind,phase:'impact',from:'player',start:performance.now(),duration:410,seed:Math.random()*200});this.v343Named=this.v343Named.slice(-10);}return ret;};
const baseProjectile=BattleEngine.prototype.drawProjectile;
BattleEngine.prototype.drawProjectile=function(p){if(!p?.v343Signature)return baseProjectile.call(this,p);const tail=p.tail?.[0]||{x:p.x-(p.vx||0)*.045,y:p.y-(p.vy||0)*.045};const q=vfxQuality(this);signatureVfx(this.ctx,p.v343Signature,'travel',tail.x,tail.y,p.x,p.y,.75,.95,p.x*.13+p.y*.17,q);};
const baseDraw=BattleEngine.prototype.draw;
BattleEngine.prototype.draw=function(now){baseDraw.call(this,now);if(!battle?.active||!this.ctx||!this.player||!this.enemy)return;const fx=this.v343Named||[];if(!fx.length)return;const ctx=this.ctx,q=vfxQuality(this);ctx.save();for(const e of fx){const dur=e.duration||350,t=q01((now-e.start)/dur);if(t>=1)continue;const source=e.from==='enemy'?this.enemy:this.player,target=e.from==='enemy'?this.player:this.enemy;signatureVfx(ctx,e.kind,e.phase,source.x,source.y-this.h*.13,target.x,target.y-this.h*.11,t,(1-t*.8)*(q==='low'?.72:1),e.seed,q);}ctx.restore();this.v343Named=fx.filter(e=>now-e.start<e.duration).slice(-8);};
// V34.3 base already uses a single FX queue. Do not allocate timers, DOM nodes or requestAnimationFrame per attack.
window.PokemonTorreV343SignatureDiagnostics=()=>({signatureCount:Object.keys(SIGNATURES).length,eleven:['thunderbolt','thunder','flamethrower','fireblast','surf','hydropump','icebeam','psychic','shadowball','earthquake','rockslide'],quality:battle?.active&&engine?vfxQuality(engine):'auto',queue:engine?.v343Named?.length||0});
})();

const MAX_FX=12;
function normalize(n){return String(n||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');}
const ALIASES={'lancachamas':'flamethrower','rajadadefogo':'fireblast','raio':'thunderbolt','trovão':'thunder','hidrobomba':'hydropump','terremoto':'earthquake','deslizamentodepedra':'rockslide','boladesombra':'shadowball','raiodegelo':'icebeam'};
function spec(n,type){
 const key=normalize(n),m=MOVES[ALIASES[key]||key];
 return m?{...m,name:n}:{...(LOOK[type]||LOOK.normal),type:type||'normal',name:n,size:1,duration:450};
}
function drawSprite(ctx,x,y,spec,t,opacity,size,now){
 const asset=SHEETS[spec.sheet]||SHEETS.spark;
 if(!asset?.ready||!asset.image.naturalWidth)return false;
 const image=asset.image,count=asset.frames;
 const srcW=image.naturalWidth/count,srcH=image.naturalHeight;
 if(!srcW||!srcH)return false;
 const frame=Math.max(0,Math.min(count-1,Math.floor(t*count)));
 const naturalScale=Math.min(1.7,Math.max(.75,size||1));
 const maxW=Math.max(38,Math.min(90,ctx.canvas.width/(window.devicePixelRatio||1)*.22));
 const base=maxW*naturalScale;
 const tall=spec.sheet==='lightning',ratio=Math.max(.42,Math.min(2.3,srcH/srcW));
 const destW=tall?base*.70:base,destH=tall?base*1.7:base*Math.min(1.30,ratio);
 ctx.save();
 ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
 ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(.94,Math.max(0,opacity));
 ctx.filter=spec.filter||(LOOK[spec.type]||LOOK.normal).filter||'none';
 ctx.drawImage(image,frame*srcW,0,srcW,srcH,x-destW*.5,y-destH*.56,destW,destH);
 ctx.restore();
 return true;
}
function addFx(engine,entry){
 engine.v344Fx ||= [];
 engine.v344Fx.push(entry);
 if(engine.v344Fx.length>MAX_FX)engine.v344Fx.splice(0,engine.v344Fx.length-MAX_FX);
}
const prevUse=BattleEngine.prototype.useMove;
BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){
 const b=battle,m=b?.active?current():null,move=m?skillSet(m)?.[i]:null;
 const now=performance.now(),before=(cooldowns||[])[i]||0;
 const out=prevUse.call(this,i);
 if(b?.active&&b===battle&&move&&cooldowns[i]>now&&cooldowns[i]!==before){
   const sp=spec(move.name,move.type),strength=move.kind==='status'?.75:1;
   addFx(this,{kind:'cast',spec:sp,from:'player',start:now,duration:sp.duration*strength,
      pulse:move.kind==='status',size:sp.size||1});
   this.v344LastMove={name:move.name,type:move.type,time:now};
 }
 return out;
};
const prevHit=BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget;
BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget=function(who,type,power){
 const before=performance.now();
 const out=prevHit.call(this,who,type,power);
 if(battle?.active){
  const lm=this.v344LastMove;
  const name=who==='enemy'&&lm?.type===type&&before-lm.time<2600?lm.name:'';
  const s=spec(name,type);
  addFx(this,{kind:'impact',spec:s,from:who==='enemy'?'player':'enemy',start:before,duration:s.duration*.85,size:s.size});
 }
 return out;
};
const prevProjectile=BattleEngine.prototype.drawProjectile;
BattleEngine.prototype.drawProjectile=function(p){
 const type=p?.type||'normal';
 if(!LOOK[type])return prevProjectile.call(this,p);
 const src=p.tail?.[0]||{x:p.x-(p.vx||0)*.045,y:p.y-(p.vy||0)*.045};
 const lm=this.v344LastMove;
 const s=spec(p.from==='player'&&lm?.type===type&&performance.now()-lm.time<1700?lm.name:'',type);
 const t=(performance.now()/Math.max(190,s.duration))%1;
 if(!drawSprite(this.ctx,p.x,p.y,s,t,.84,s.size*.55,performance.now()))return prevProjectile.call(this,p);
};
const prevDraw=BattleEngine.prototype.draw;
BattleEngine.prototype.draw=function(now){
 const hold={v342Lightning:this.v342Lightning,v343Fx:this.v343Fx,v343Named:this.v343Named,v30Shots:this.v30Shots,v19Beams:this.v19Beams,v19Impacts:this.v19Impacts};
 this.v342Lightning=[];this.v343Fx=[];this.v343Named=[];
 this.v30Shots=[];this.v19Beams=[];this.v19Impacts=[];
 try{prevDraw.call(this,now);}finally{
  this.v342Lightning=hold.v342Lightning;this.v343Fx=hold.v343Fx;this.v343Named=hold.v343Named;
  this.v30Shots=hold.v30Shots;this.v19Beams=hold.v19Beams;this.v19Impacts=hold.v19Impacts;
 }
 if(!battle?.active||!this.ctx)return;
 const ctx=this.ctx,low=this.fps<30;
 ctx.save();
 const list=this.v344Fx||[];
 for(const e of list){
  const time=now-e.start,frac=time/e.duration;
  if(frac>=1||frac<0)continue;
  const p=e.from==='enemy'?this.enemy:this.player;
  const dest=e.from==='enemy'?this.player:this.enemy;
  const x=e.kind==='impact'?dest.x:p.x+(dest.x-p.x)*Math.min(1,frac*1.5),
        y=e.kind==='impact'?dest.y-this.h*.16:p.y-this.h*.12+(dest.y-p.y)*Math.min(1,frac*1.5);
  const a=frac<.12?frac/.12:frac>.82?(1-frac)/.18:1;
  if(low&&e!==list[list.length-1])continue;
  drawSprite(ctx,x,y,e.spec,frac,a*.88,e.size*(e.kind==='impact'?1.1:.83),now);
 }
 // Homing e disparos especiais: mesmas spritesheets, sem a bolinha original.
 for(const p of (hold.v30Shots||[]).slice(-8)){
  const lm=this.v344LastMove;
  const s=spec(lm?.type===p.type?lm.name:'',p.type);
  drawSprite(ctx,p.x,p.y,s,(now/Math.max(170,s.duration))%1,.75,s.size*.49,now);
 }
 ctx.restore();
 this.v344Fx=list.filter(e=>now-e.start<e.duration).slice(-MAX_FX);
};
const css=document.createElement('style');
css.id='v344_sprite_styles';css.textContent='.skillV8 .skillIcon{filter:none!important;image-rendering:pixelated}.skillV8.kind-status .skillIcon{opacity:.96}';
document.head.appendChild(css);
window.PokemonTorreV344Diagnostics=()=>({version:V344,realSpriteSheets:Object.keys(SHEETS),
 loaded:Object.entries(SHEETS).filter(([,s])=>s.ready).map(([k])=>k),
 types:Object.keys(LOOK),namedMoves:Object.keys(MOVES),queue:engine?.v344Fx?.length||0});
})();

  }

function flag(n){return (Math.sin(n*13.345+1.75)*14758.234)%1;}
function rnd(n){let x=flag(n);return x<0?x+1:x;}
function stroke(ctx,p,clr,width,alpha){if(p.length<2)return;ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.strokeStyle=clr;ctx.lineWidth=width;ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p[0][0],p[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<p.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(p[i][0],p[i][1]);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
function bolt(ctx,x1,y1,x2,y2,seed,progress,alpha){
 const dx=x2-x1,dy=y2-y1,l=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,px=-dy/l,py=dx/l;
 const a=[[x1,y1]],segments=12,ends=clamp(Math.ceil(segments*progress),1,segments);
 for(let i=1;i<=ends;i++){const t=i/segments,off=(rnd(seed+i*7)-.5)*Math.min(27,l*.16)*Math.sin(Math.PI*t);a.push([x1+dx*t+px*off,y1+dy*t+py*off]);}
 stroke(ctx,a,'#4257ad',6,alpha*.28);stroke(ctx,a,'#f7d56a',3.0,alpha*.82);stroke(ctx,a,'#fff9cd',1.15,alpha);
 for(let i=2;i<a.length-1;i+=3){const p=a[i],sg=i%2?1:-1;stroke(ctx,[p,[p[0]+px*l*.105*sg+dx*.05,p[1]+py*l*.105*sg+dy*.05],[p[0]+px*l*.17*sg+dx*.08,p[1]+py*l*.17*sg+dy*.08]],'#fff7ae',1.15,alpha*.78);}
}

  for(let k=0;k<6;k++){const angle=k*1.047+mark.seed*.06,points=[[x,y]];for(let j=1;j<=3;j++){const r=j*(10+mark.power*.13),tw=(rnd(mark.seed+k*8+j)-.5)*8;points.push([x+Math.cos(angle)*r+tw,y+Math.sin(angle)*r*.55+tw*.30]);}stroke(ctx,points,'#49382c',2.2,a);stroke(ctx,points,'#e8c29b',.8,a*.55);}
 }else if(mark.type==='fireblast'||mark.type==='flame'){

 }else if(mark.type==='poison'||mark.type==='shadow'){
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=a*.42;ctx.fillStyle='#8651a4';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y,mark.power*.48,mark.power*.22,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
 }else if(mark.type==='surf'||mark.type==='pump'){
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=a*.23;ctx.fillStyle='#8fdef4';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y,mark.power*.54,mark.power*.20,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
 }
}
function drawAttack(ctx,e,now,engine){
 const t=clamp((now-e.at)/e.duration,0,1),a=clamp(Math.min(t*5,(1-t)*5,1),0,1),w=engine.w,h=engine.h;
 const x=e.x1,y=e.y1,X=e.x2,Y=e.y2,dx=X-x,dy=Y-y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,perp=[-dy/len,dx/len];
 const tip=[x+dx*clamp(t*1.65,0,1),y+dy*clamp(t*1.65,0,1)];
 if(e.kind==='bolt'){bolt(ctx,x,y,X,Y,e.seed,t*1.7,a);return;}
 if(e.kind==='thunder'){
  const startX=X+Math.sin(e.seed)*h*.03;
  bolt(ctx,startX,-h*.05,X,Y,e.seed,t*1.5,a);bolt(ctx,startX+16,-h*.12,X+8,Y-12,e.seed+3,t*1.4,a*.58);return;
 }
 if(e.kind==='flame'||e.kind==='fireblast'){
  const jets=e.kind==='fireblast'?7:5,angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
  for(let j=0;j<jets;j++){const q=(j-(jets-1)/2)/(jets-1),rad=angle+q*(e.kind==='fireblast'?.68:.24),dist=len*clamp(t*1.55,0,1)*(e.kind==='fireblast'?.8+Math.abs(q)*.32:1);
    const p=[x+Math.cos(rad)*dist,y+Math.sin(rad)*dist];const mid=[x+Math.cos(rad)*dist*.58+perp[0]*Math.sin(now*.011+j)*5,y+Math.sin(rad)*dist*.58+perp[1]*Math.sin(now*.011+j)*5];
    stroke(ctx,[[x,y],mid,p],j%2?'#ff7130':'#ffc458',2.5+(1-Math.abs(q))*5,a*.78);
    if(j%2===0&&t>.23){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=a*.8;ctx.fillStyle='#ffd685';ctx.fillRect(p[0]-2,p[1]-2,4,4);ctx.restore();}
  }return;
 }
 if(e.kind==='surf'||e.kind==='pump'){
  const n=e.kind==='surf'?4:2;
  for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const offset=(j-(n-1)/2)*(e.kind==='surf'?10:5),p=[];
   for(let k=0;k<=9;k++){const q=k/9*t*1.6;p.push([x+dx*clamp(q,0,1)+perp[0]*(offset+Math.sin(q*17+now*.008+j)*5),y+dy*clamp(q,0,1)+perp[1]*(offset+Math.sin(q*17+now*.008+j)*5)]);}
   stroke(ctx,p,j%2?'#e4ffff':'#68caff',e.kind==='surf'?6:3.6,a*.75);
  }return;
 }
 if(e.kind==='ice'){
  stroke(ctx,[[x,y],[tip[0],tip[1]]],'#d6fbff',5,a*.68);
  for(let j=0;j<12;j++){const q=j/12*t*1.6,c=[x+dx*clamp(q,0,1),y+dy*clamp(q,0,1)],r=4+rnd(j+e.seed)*5;
   stroke(ctx,[[c[0]-r,c[1]],[c[0]+r,c[1]],[c[0],c[1]-r],[c[0],c[1]+r]],'#eaffff',.8,a*.8);
  }return;
 }
 if(e.kind==='psychic'||e.kind==='shadow'){
  for(let j=0;j<4;j++){const tt=clamp(t*1.3-j*.13,0,1),cx=x+dx*tt,cy=y+dy*tt;
   ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=a*.62;ctx.strokeStyle=e.kind==='psychic'?'#db8cff':'#7c63b6';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();
   ctx.ellipse(cx,cy,9+j*4,5+j*3,t*4+j,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
  }return;
 }
 if(e.kind==='quake'||e.kind==='rocks'){
  for(let j=0;j<7;j++){const q=j/6,px=X+(rnd(e.seed+j*3)-.5)*h*.22,py=Y+(rnd(e.seed+j*8)-.5)*h*.12;
   if(e.kind==='quake'){
    if(t>q*.15)stroke(ctx,[[px-8,py+2],[px+1,py-6],[px+8,py+4]],'#f4bd7c',2.8,a*.73);
   }else{
    const yy=py-h*.45*(1-t)+Math.sin(now*.015+j)*2;
    ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=a*.74;ctx.fillStyle=j%2?'#b88b58':'#e6c298';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(px-6,yy);ctx.lineTo(px,yy-8);ctx.lineTo(px+7,yy-2);ctx.lineTo(px+2,yy+7);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
   }
  }return;
 }
}
function atmosphere(engine,now){
 const c=engine.ctx,w=engine.w,h=engine.h,mode=env(),low=engine.fps<32;
 if(mode==='clear')return;
 c.save();
 if(mode==='fog'){
  for(let j=0;j<(low?3:6);j++){const x=((now*(j%2?-.009:.011)+j*154)%(w+180)+w+180)%(w+180)-90;
    const y=h*(.20+j*.125),g=c.createLinearGradient(x-110,y,x+110,y);
    g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(217,228,239,0)');g.addColorStop(.5,'rgba(215,234,239,.10)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(217,229,239,0)');
    c.fillStyle=g;c.beginPath();c.ellipse(x,y,105,20+j*2,0,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill();
  }
 }else if(mode==='volcano'){
  const groundG=c.createLinearGradient(0,h*.65,0,h);
  groundG.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,99,31,0)');groundG.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,83,9,.15)');
  c.fillStyle=groundG;c.fillRect(0,h*.65,w,h*.35);
  for(let i=0;i<(low?9:18);i++){const phase=(now*.000065*(.6+i%4*.23)+rnd(i*19.5))%1;
   const x=(rnd(i*7.2)*w+Math.sin(now*.001+i)*8),y=h*(.92-phase*.68),sz=1.2+(i%3);
   c.globalAlpha=(1-phase)*.64;c.fillStyle=i%3===0?'#fff4a1':i%3===1?'#ff9a41':'#f35b2b';c.fillRect(x,y,sz,sz);
  }c.globalAlpha=1;
 }else if(mode==='sun'){
  const g=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,h);
  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,222,125,.14)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,223,125,0)');
  c.fillStyle=g;c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
  for(let i=0;i<(low?2:4);i++){const drift=Math.sin(now*.00025+i)*w*.025;
   c.fillStyle='rgba(255,245,182,.045)';c.beginPath();c.moveTo(w*(.60+i*.085)+drift,0);
   c.lineTo(w*(.78+i*.09)+drift,0);c.lineTo(w*(.55+i*.12)+drift,h);c.lineTo(w*(.41+i*.09)+drift,h);c.fill();}
 }else if(mode==='rain'||mode==='storm'){
  c.strokeStyle='rgba(181,225,249,.22)';c.lineWidth=1;
  for(let i=0;i<(low?12:26);i++){const x=(rnd(i*13)*w+now*.06)%w,y=(rnd(i*17)*h+now*.19)%h;
   c.beginPath();c.moveTo(x,y);c.lineTo(x-5,y+12);c.stroke();}
 }else if(mode==='sand'){
  c.fillStyle='rgba(223,174,109,.07)';c.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
 }
 c.restore();
}
function orderEditor(){
 const list=document.querySelector('#modal .v331MoveList');if(!list)return false;
 const items=[...list.querySelectorAll(':scope > .v331Choice')];if(!items.length)return false;
 const num=(str,re)=>{const match=String(str||'').match(re);return match?Number(match[1]):0;};
 const records=items.map((element,index)=>{
   const small=element.querySelector('small')?.textContent||'';
   const cost=num(small,/(\d+)\s*energia/i),power=num(small,/(\d+)\s*força/i),cool=num(small,/([\d.,]+)\s*s\b/i);
   return {element,index,cost,power,cool};
 });
 records.sort((a,b)=>(a.cost===0?0:1)-(b.cost===0?0:1)||b.power-a.power||a.cost-b.cost||a.cool-b.cool||a.index-b.index);
 for(const item of records)list.appendChild(item.element);
 list.setAttribute('data-v346-sorted','1');
 return true;
}
const sortObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>{const list=document.querySelector('#modal .v331MoveList');if(list&&list.getAttribute('data-v346-sorted')!=='1')orderEditor();});
sortObserver.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
const prevUse=BattleEngine.prototype.useMove;
BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){
 const b=battle,m=b?.active?current():null,s=m?skillSet(m)?.[i]:null,now=performance.now(),prior=cooldowns[i]||0;
 const result=prevUse.call(this,i);
 if(s&&b===battle&&b?.active&&cooldowns[i]>now&&cooldowns[i]!==prior){
  const style=fxStyles[nrm(s.name)];
  if(style){
   const src=this.player,dst=this.enemy;
   this.v346Attacks||=[];this.v346Attacks.push({kind:style.kind,at:now,duration:style.duration,seed:Math.random()*1000,x1:src.x,y1:src.y-this.h*.1,x2:dst.x,y2:dst.y-this.h*.08});
   if(this.v346Attacks.length>7)this.v346Attacks.shift();
  }
 }
 return result;
};
const prevHit=BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget;
BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget=function(who,type,power){
 const target=who==='enemy'?this.enemy:this.player,now=performance.now(),v=this.v346Attacks?.[this.v346Attacks.length-1],result=prevHit.call(this,who,type,power);
 if(target&&battle?.active&&who==='enemy'&&v&&now-v.at<1400&&['quake','rocks','fireblast','flame','surf','pump','shadow'].includes(v.kind)){
  this.v346Marks||=[];this.v346Marks.push({type:v.kind,x:target.x,y:target.y+this.h*.06,at:now,life:3500,power:this.h*.095,seed:v.seed});
  if(this.v346Marks.length>15)this.v346Marks.shift();
 }
 return result;
};
const prevDrawMon=BattleEngine.prototype.drawMon;
BattleEngine.prototype.drawMon=function(actor,id,isEnemy,now){
 if(this.v346Marks&&this.v346GroundAt!==now){
   this.v346GroundAt=now;
   const ctx=this.ctx;ctx.save();
   for(const m of this.v346Marks)ground(ctx,m,now);
   ctx.restore();
 }
 return prevDrawMon.call(this,actor,id,isEnemy,now);
};
const prevDraw=BattleEngine.prototype.draw;
BattleEngine.prototype.draw=function(now){
 prevDraw.call(this,now);
 if(!battle?.active||!this.ctx)return;
 this.v346Marks=(this.v346Marks||[]).filter(m=>now-m.at<m.life);
 atmosphere(this,now);
 const ctx=this.ctx;ctx.save();
 const animations=this.v346Attacks||[],low=this.fps<30;
 for(const a of animations.slice(low?-2:-5))if(now-a.at<a.duration)drawAttack(ctx,a,now,this);
 ctx.restore();
 this.v346Attacks=animations.filter(a=>now-a.at<a.duration);
};
const prevStart=startBattle;
startBattle=function(enemies,label,wild){
 const result=prevStart(enemies,label,wild);
 if(battle?.active){
  if(/nevoeiro|fog|mist|bruma/i.test(label||''))battle.v32Weather='fog';
  if(engine){engine.v346Attacks=[];engine.v346Marks=[];}
 }
 return result;
};
const css=document.createElement('style');css.id='v346-design';css.textContent=
 '.v331MoveList .v331Choice{min-height:52px!important;padding:8px 10px!important;gap:7px!important}' +
 '.v331Choice b{font-size:11px!important}.v331Choice small{font-size:9px!important;line-height:1.25!important}' +
 '.v331Choice .pillSmall{font-size:9px!important;min-width:53px!important}' +
 '.v331MoveList[data-v346-sorted="1"]{scroll-behavior:smooth}' +
 '@media(max-width:480px){.v331Slots{gap:5px!important}.v331Slot{padding:8px!important}.v331Slot b{font-size:11px!important}.v331Slot small{font-size:9px!important}.v331MoveList .v331Choice{min-height:49px!important;padding:7px 9px!important}}';
document.head.appendChild(css);
window.PokemonTorreV346Diagnostics=()=>({version:REV,weather:env(),environments:Object.keys(THEME_NAMES),signatureMoves:Object.keys(fxStyles),sortEnabled:!!document.querySelector('[data-v346-sorted="1"]'),activeFx:engine?.v346Attacks?.length||0,marks:engine?.v346Marks?.length||0});
})();

/* Pokémon Torre V34.7 — seleção de sprites dos jogos referenciados */
(function(){
'use strict';
const ROOT='https://raw.githubusercontent.com/PokeAPI/sprites/master/sprites/pokemon/versions/generation-iii/';
const GAMES=['firered-leafgreen','emerald','ruby-sapphire'];
const EMERALD=new Set([3,6,9,12,18,26,31,34,38,45,49,59,65,94,95,103,110,121,130,131,134,135,136,143,144,145,146,149,150,151]);
const RUBY=new Set([24,36,40,53,55,62,73,87,97,112,113,115,122,124,125,126,132,138,139,140,141,142,147,148]);
const byName={};
for(let i=1;i<=151;i++){
 const n=D[i]?.name;
 if(n)byName[n.toLocaleLowerCase('pt-BR')]=i;
}
function edition(id){
 if(EMERALD.has(Number(id)))return 'emerald';
 if(RUBY.has(Number(id)))return 'ruby-sapphire';
 return 'firered-leafgreen';
}
function urls(id,shiny){
 const main=edition(id),all=[main,...GAMES.filter(x=>x!==main)];
 return all.map(game=>ROOT+game+'/'+(shiny?'shiny/':'')+id+'.png');
}
function ready(pic){return Boolean(pic?.complete&&pic.naturalWidth>0);}
function loadGameImage(id,game,shiny){
 const image=new Image();image.decoding='async';image.crossOrigin='anonymous';
 image.src=ROOT+game+'/'+(shiny?'shiny/':'')+id+'.png';
 return image;
}
const loaded=new Map();
function selection(id,shiny=false){
 id=Number(id);const key=id+(shiny?'s':'n');if(loaded.has(key))return loaded.get(key);
 const games=[edition(id),...GAMES.filter(x=>x!==edition(id))];
 const info={selected:games[0],image:null,failures:0,ready:false};
 function next(k){
  if(k>=games.length){info.image=null;return;}
  const img=loadGameImage(id,games[k],shiny);info.image=img;info.selected=games[k];
  img.onload=()=>{info.ready=ready(img)};
  img.onerror=()=>{info.failures++;next(k+1)};
  if(ready(img))info.ready=true;
 }
 next(0);loaded.set(key,info);return info;
}
// A arena usa prioritariamente as animações de Mystery Dungeon (andar, atacar, levar golpe).
// Não elimina os sprites locais: se a conexão falhar, continua com o visual atual.
const previousDraw=BattleEngine.prototype.drawMon;
BattleEngine.prototype.drawMon=function(actor,id,enemy,now){
 const e=this.assets?.get(id);
 if(!e)return previousDraw.call(this,actor,id,enemy,now);
 const special=enemy?foe():current(),shiny=Boolean(special?.shiny||special?.isShiny);
 const action=actor.flash>0?'Hurt':(this.attackActs?.[enemy?'enemy':'player']||0)>this.time?'Attack':
   (Math.hypot(actor.x-(actor._animX||actor.x),actor.y-(actor._animY||actor.y))>.4?'Walk':'Idle');
 const sh=e.sheets?.[action],md=e.meta?.[action];
 const idle=e.sheets?.Idle,im=e.meta?.Idle;
 const pmdAvailable=(ready(sh)&&md?.width>0&&md?.height>0)||(ready(idle)&&im?.width>0&&im?.height>0);
 const keepCustom=e.custom,keepStatic=e.customStatic,keepArt=e.art;
 if(pmdAvailable){
   // Mantém a animação PMD no combate para não virar um PNG estático/quadrado.
   // A cor shiny será aplicada depois pelo renderizador dedicado.
   e.custom={};
   e.customStatic=null;
 }else if(!shiny){
   // Para monstros normais, se a spritesheet falhar, ainda pode usar o sprite clássico.
   const chosen=selection(id,false);
   if(chosen.ready&&ready(chosen.image)){
     e.custom={};
     e.customStatic=chosen.image;
   }
 }
 try{return previousDraw.call(this,actor,id,enemy,now);}
 finally{e.custom=keepCustom;e.customStatic=keepStatic;e.art=keepArt;}
};
// As fichas, Pokédex e equipe recebem sprites clássicos HD-pixel conforme a espécie.
function enhancePortrait(pic,forceId=null,shiny=false){
 if(!pic)return;
 const name=(pic.getAttribute('alt')||'').toLocaleLowerCase('pt-BR'),id=Number(forceId||byName[name]||0);
 if(!id)return;
 const key=id+'-'+(shiny?'s':'n');
 if(pic.dataset.v347Processed===key)return;
 pic.dataset.v347Processed=key;
 const fallback=pic.getAttribute('src')||'';
 const seq=urls(id,shiny),ref={index:0};
 pic.onerror=()=>{
   ref.index+=1;
   if(ref.index<seq.length)pic.src=seq[ref.index];
   else if(fallback && !pic.dataset.v347Fallback){pic.dataset.v347Fallback='1';pic.src=fallback;}
 };
 pic.src=seq[0];
 pic.style.imageRendering='pixelated';
 pic.style.objectFit='contain';
 pic.dataset.v347Source=edition(id)+(shiny?'-shiny':'');
}
function v3555RefreshShinyPortraits(){
 document.querySelectorAll('.moncard').forEach((card,i)=>{const pic=card.querySelector('.spritewrap img.sprite');const m=(save?.team||[])[i];if(pic&&m)enhancePortrait(pic,m.id,!!(m.shiny||m.isShiny));});
 document.querySelectorAll('.list [data-team]').forEach(btn=>{const pic=btn.querySelector('.spritewrap img.sprite');const idx=Number(btn.dataset.team);const all=[...(save?.team||[]),...(save?.box||[])];const m=all[idx];if(pic&&m)enhancePortrait(pic,m.id,!!(m.shiny||m.isShiny));});
 document.querySelectorAll('.swapgrid [data-swap]').forEach(btn=>{const pic=btn.querySelector('.spritewrap img.sprite');const idx=Number(btn.dataset.swap);const m=(save?.team||[])[idx];if(pic&&m)enhancePortrait(pic,m.id,!!(m.shiny||m.isShiny));});
 document.querySelectorAll('.spritewrap img.sprite').forEach(pic=>{if(!pic.dataset.v347Processed)enhancePortrait(pic);});
}
let scheduled=false;
function refresh(){
 v3555RefreshShinyPortraits();
 const team=document.querySelectorAll('.moncard .spritewrap img.sprite');
 for(const pic of team)pic.style.imageRendering='pixelated';
}
const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>{
 if(scheduled)return;scheduled=true;
 queueMicrotask(()=>{scheduled=false;refresh()});
});
observer.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
refresh();
const style=document.createElement('style');
style.id='v347PokemonSpriteStyle';
style.textContent='.spritewrap .sprite{object-fit:contain!important;image-rendering:pixelated!important}.moncard .spritewrap{overflow:visible}.dexCard .spritewrap .sprite{image-rendering:pixelated!important}';
document.head.appendChild(style);
window.PokemonTorreV347SpriteDiagnostics=()=>({
 version:'34.7-sprites',
 games:GAMES,
 mainSource:'Mystery Dungeon / PMDCollab — em movimento',
 alternatives:['FireRed / LeafGreen','Emerald','Ruby / Sapphire'],
 mappedSpecies:151,
 preferences:{emerald:[...EMERALD].length,ruby:[...RUBY].length,firered:151-EMERALD.size-RUBY.size},
 currentTeam:(save?.team||[]).map(m=>({id:m.id,edition:edition(m.id),shiny:Boolean(m.shiny||m.isShiny)})),
 portraits:document.querySelectorAll('[data-v347-processed]').length
});
})();

/* V34.7 — efeitos clássicos FireRed em PNG, com fallback ao motor atual */
(function(){
'use strict';
const CDN='https://raw.githubusercontent.com/pret/pokefirered/master/graphics/battle_anims/sprites/';
const TYPE_SHEETS={
 fire:['fire_plume','small_ember','explosion'],water:['water_column','water_impact','bubble_burst'],
 grass:['razor_leaf','vine','roots'],ground:['flying_dirt','mud_sand_0','dirt_mound'],
 rock:['rocks','flat_rock','impact'],ice:['ice_crystals_0','ice_spikes','ice_chunk'],
 poison:['poison_powder','poison_bubble','toxic_bubble'],psychic:['spiral','rainbow_rings','movement_waves'],
 ghost:['shadow_ball','ghostly_spirit','purple_flame'],dark:['black_smoke','void_lines','purple_swipe'],
 flying:['air_wave','gust','white_feather'],bug:['spider_web','needle','web_thread'],
 normal:['impact','slash','speed_dust'],fighting:['punch_impact','slam_hit','claw_slash'],
 steel:['torn_metal','metal_ball','shock_3'],dragon:['blue_flames','breath','blue_burst'],
 fairy:['sparkle_4','pink_heart_2','pink_cloud']
};
const SPECIAL={
 'lancachamas':'fire_plume','flamethrower':'fire_plume','explosaodefogo':'explosion',
 'fireblast':'explosion','brasa':'small_ember','ember':'small_ember','surf':'water_column',
 'hydropump':'water_impact','hidrobomba':'water_impact','raiofolha':'razor_leaf',
 'razorleaf':'razor_leaf','vinewhip':'vine','chicotedevinhas':'vine',
 'earthquake':'dirt_mound','terremoto':'dirt_mound',
 'osso bumerangue':'bone','ossobumerangue':'bone','bonemerang':'bone',
 'sludgebomb':'poison_bubble','sleep powder':'poison_powder','sleeppowder':'poison_powder',
 'toxic':'toxic_bubble','icebeam':'ice_spikes','raiodegelo':'ice_spikes',
 'shadowball':'shadow_ball','boladesombra':'shadow_ball','psychic':'spiral',
 'psi quico':'spiral','psíquico':'spiral','rockslide':'rocks','deslizamentodepedra':'rocks',
 'tapa de lama':'mud_sand_0','tapadelama':'mud_sand_0','sandattack':'flying_dirt'
};
function norm(s){return String(s||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9]/ig,'').toLowerCase();}
const MAP={};for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(SPECIAL))MAP[norm(k)]=v;
const files=[...new Set(Object.values(TYPE_SHEETS).flat().concat(Object.values(MAP),['shock_2','ice_chunk','fire_plume','toxic_bubble','snore_z','spiral']))];
const art={};
for(const name of files){
 const pic=new Image();pic.decoding='async';pic.crossOrigin='anonymous';
 pic.src=CDN+name+'.png';art[name]=pic;
}
function loaded(a){return !!(a&&a.complete&&a.naturalWidth>0&&a.naturalHeight>0);}
function choose(move,type,impact=false){
 const name=norm(move),special=MAP[name];
 if(special)return special;
 const list=TYPE_SHEETS[type]||TYPE_SHEETS.normal;
 return list[impact?1:0]||list[0];
}
function frame(ctx,name,x,y,progress,size=1,alpha=.9,rotation=0){
 const pic=art[name];if(!loaded(pic))return false;
 const sw=pic.naturalWidth,sh=pic.naturalHeight;
 const count=sh>=sw&&sh%sw===0?Math.max(1,Math.min(32,sh/sw)):1;
 const sy=sw*Math.floor(Math.max(0,Math.min(.999,progress))*count),height=count>1?sw:sh;
 const target=Math.max(14,Math.min(64,36*size)),scale=Math.min(1.55,height/Math.max(1,sw));
 ctx.save();ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,Math.min(.94,alpha));
 ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(rotation);
 ctx.drawImage(pic,0,sy,sw,height,-target/2,-target*scale/2,target,target*scale);
 ctx.restore();return true;
}
function add(e,fx){
 e.v347Sprites||=[];
 e.v347Sprites.push(fx);
 if(e.v347Sprites.length>28)e.v347Sprites.splice(0,e.v347Sprites.length-28);
}
const oldUse=BattleEngine.prototype.useMove;
BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){
 const b=battle,m=b?.active?current():null,s=m&&skillSet(m)?.[i];
 const now=performance.now(),before=(cooldowns||[])[i]||0;
 const res=oldUse.call(this,i);
 if(s?.type!=='electric'&&b?.active&&b===battle&&(cooldowns||[])[i]>now&&(cooldowns||[])[i]!==before){
  const a=this.player,z=this.enemy;
  add(this,{kind:'travel',name:choose(s.name,s.type),type:s.type,at:now,duration:s.kind==='beam'?690:490,
     x1:a.x,y1:a.y-this.h*.18,x2:z.x,y2:z.y-this.h*.16,seed:Math.random()*100,move:s.name});
  this.v347Last={move:s.name,type:s.type,at:now};
 }
 return res;
};
const oldHit=BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget;
BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget=function(side,type,power){
 const result=oldHit.call(this,side,type,power);
 if(battle?.active&&type!=='electric'){
  const recent=this.v347Last;
  const name=recent?.type===type&&performance.now()-recent.at<2000?recent.move:'';
  const target=side==='enemy'?this.player:this.enemy,now=performance.now();
  if(target)add(this,{kind:'impact',name:choose(name,type,true),type,at:now,duration:340,
    x:target.x,y:target.y-this.h*.14});
 }
 return result;
};
const oldProjectile=BattleEngine.prototype.drawProjectile;
BattleEngine.prototype.drawProjectile=function(p){
 if(p.type==='electric')return oldProjectile.call(this,p);
 if(p.type&&TYPE_SHEETS[p.type]){
  const recent=this.v347Last,match=recent?.type===p.type&&performance.now()-recent.at<1700;
  const file=choose(match?recent.move:'',p.type,false);
  const direction=Math.atan2(p.vy||0,p.vx||1);
  if(frame(this.ctx,file,p.x,p.y,(performance.now()/360)%1,.68,.86,direction))return;
 }
 return oldProjectile.call(this,p);
};
const oldDraw=BattleEngine.prototype.draw;
BattleEngine.prototype.draw=function(now){
 // Remove só os ataques genéricos NÃO elétricos das camadas anteriores.
 const old346=this.v346Attacks,old343=this.v343Fx,old344=this.v344Fx,oldNamed=this.v343Named;
 if(old346)this.v346Attacks=old346.filter(x=>x.kind==='bolt'||x.kind==='thunder');
 if(old343)this.v343Fx=old343.filter(x=>x.type==='electric');
 if(old344)this.v344Fx=old344.filter(x=>x.spec?.type==='electric');
 if(oldNamed)this.v343Named=oldNamed.filter(x=>x.type==='electric');
 try{oldDraw.call(this,now);}finally{
  if(old346)this.v346Attacks=old346.filter(x=>now-x.at<x.duration);
  if(old343)this.v343Fx=old343.filter(x=>now<x.end);
  if(old344)this.v344Fx=old344.filter(x=>now-x.start<x.duration);
  if(oldNamed)this.v343Named=oldNamed;
 }
 if(!battle?.active)return;
 const ctx=this.ctx,low=!!this.fps&&this.fps<29;
 const src=this.v347Sprites||[];
 for(const v of src.slice(low?-6:-18)){
  const t=(now-v.at)/v.duration;
  if(t<0||t>=1)continue;
  if(v.kind==='travel'){
   const t1=Math.min(1,t*1.6),x=v.x1+(v.x2-v.x1)*t1,y=v.y1+(v.y2-v.y1)*t1;
   frame(ctx,v.name,x,y,t,low?.80:1,t<.85?.9:(1-t)*5,Math.atan2(v.y2-v.y1,v.x2-v.x1));
  }else if(v.kind==='impact')frame(ctx,v.name,v.x,v.y,t,1.18,(1-t)*.9);
 }
 this.v347Sprites=src.filter(x=>now-x.at<x.duration);
 // Condições de status visíveis no corpo, sem alterar HP/dano/cooldowns.
 const state=this.v25?.stats;
 if(state){
  const sides=[{actor:this.player,conditions:state.player?.conditions||[]},
    {actor:this.enemy,conditions:state.enemy?.conditions||[]}];
  for(const {actor,conditions} of sides){
   if(!actor||!conditions?.length)continue;
   for(let k=0;k<Math.min(3,conditions.length);k++){
    const status=conditions[k],image=({
      paralysis:'shock_2',frozen:'ice_chunk',freeze:'ice_chunk',
      burn:'fire_plume',poison:'toxic_bubble',sleep:'snore_z',confusion:'spiral'
    })[status];if(!image)continue;
    const phase=now*.004+k*2.1,x=actor.x+Math.sin(phase)*this.h*.055,
      y=actor.y-this.h*.17+Math.cos(phase*1.2)*this.h*.045;
    frame(ctx,image,x,y,(now/760)%1,.48,.72);
   }
  }
 }
};
window.PokemonTorreV347MoveDiagnostics=()=>({version:'34.7-FRLG-moves',sources:files.length,
  moveTypes:Object.keys(TYPE_SHEETS).length,spriteEffects:engine?.v347Sprites?.length||0,
  loaded:Object.keys(art).filter(x=>loaded(art[x])).length,lightningUntouched:true});
})();

(function(){
'use strict';
const V='35.1';
const safe=(x,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,x));
const key=s=>String(s||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');
const SKILL_STAT={atk:'ATQ',def:'DEF',speed:'VEL',accuracy:'PREC'};
const healing=new Map([['recover',.5],['roost',.5],['synthesis',.5],['moonlight',.5],['softboiled',.5],['rest',.65],['recuperacao',.5],['restauracao',.5]]);
const alias={
 growl:['debuff','atk',.15],tailwhip:['debuff','def',.16],leer:['debuff','def',.17],
 sandattack:['debuff','accuracy',.2],smokescreen:['debuff','accuracy',.2],fumaca:['debuff','accuracy',.2],stringshot:['debuff','speed',.22],
 scaryface:['debuff','speed',.23],screech:['debuff','def',.29],charm:['debuff','atk',.25],
 growth:['buff','atk',.22],agility:['buff','speed',.25],swordsdance:['buff','atk',.35],harden:['buff','def',.22],
 withdraw:['buff','def',.20],irondefense:['buff','def',.34],amnesia:['buff','def',.24],defensecurl:['buff','def',.20],
 calmmind:['double','atk','def',.18],bulkup:['double','atk','def',.2],dragondance:['double','atk','speed',.2],
 nastyplot:['buff','atk',.34],
 thunderwave:['condition','paralysis',6500],stunspore:['condition','paralysis',6500],
 sleeppowder:['condition','sleep',4800],spore:['condition','sleep',4800],hypnosis:['condition','sleep',4800],
 poisonpowder:['condition','poison',12500],toxic:['condition','poison',14000],
 willowispalt:['condition','burn',10500],willowisp:['condition','burn',10500],
 confuseray:['condition','confusion',8000],supersonic:['condition','confusion',7500],
 leechseed:['condition','seed',12000],
 protect:['shield',2.1],reflect:['buff','def',.2],lightscreen:['buff','def',.2],
 safeguard:['cleanse'],haze:['reset'],doubleteam:['dodge'],
 sunnyday:['weather','sun'],raindance:['weather','rain'],sandstorm:['weather','sand']
};
function defFor(move){
 const n=key(move.name),cfg=(window.__V351_STATUS_DEFS?.[move.name]||(typeof STATUS_DEFS!=='undefined'?STATUS_DEFS[move.name]:null));
 if(healing.has(n)||move.kind==='recover')return ['heal',healing.get(n)||.5];
 if(alias[n])return alias[n];
 if(cfg){const kind=cfg[4],d=cfg[5];
  if(['atkdown','defdown','speeddown','accuracydown'].includes(kind))return ['debuff',kind.replace('down',''),d];
  if(['atkup','defup','speedup'].includes(kind))return ['buff',kind.replace('up',''),d];
  if(kind==='mindup')return ['double','atk','def',d];
  if(kind==='bulk')return ['double','atk','def',d];
  if(kind==='dance')return ['double','atk','speed',d];
  if(['poison','paralysis','sleep','burn','confusion','seed'].includes(kind))return ['condition',kind,kind==='sleep'?4800:kind==='paralysis'?7000:11000];
  if(kind==='reflect')return ['buff','def',d];
  if(kind==='protect')return ['shield',2];
  if(kind==='cleanse')return ['cleanse'];
  if(kind==='haze')return ['reset'];
  if(kind==='evasion')return ['dodge'];
  if(['rain','sun','sand'].includes(kind))return ['weather',kind];
  if(kind==='heal')return ['heal',d];
 }
 const legacy=window.__V351_OLD_STATUS?.[move.name]||(typeof V25_STATUS!=='undefined'?V25_STATUS[move.name]:null);
 if(legacy){
  if(legacy.mode==='condition')return ['condition',legacy.effect,8800];
  if(legacy.mode==='reset')return ['reset'];
  if(legacy.mode==='buff'||legacy.mode==='debuff'){
    const stats=legacy.stat==='atkdef'?['atk','def']:legacy.stat==='atkspeed'?['atk','speed']:null;
    if(stats)return ['double',...stats,legacy.amount*(legacy.mode==='buff'?1:-1)];
    return [legacy.mode,legacy.stat,legacy.amount];
  }
 }
 if(move.kind==='shield')return ['shield',1.65];
 return null;
}
function note(engine,side,text,color){
 if(typeof engine.v26Label==='function')engine.v26Label(side,text,color,'stat');
}
const oldMove=BattleEngine.prototype.useMove;
BattleEngine.prototype.useMove=function(i){
 const b=battle,m=b?.active?current():null,move=m&&Number.isInteger(i)?skillSet(m)?.[i]:null,now=performance.now();
 if(!move||i<0||i>3||!b?.active)return oldMove.call(this,i);
 const prior=cooldowns[i]||0;
 if(prior>now+70000)cooldowns[i]=0; // proteção contra relógios misturados
 if(this.v31RecoverUntil&&this.v31RecoverUntil>now+3500)this.v31RecoverUntil=0;
 const isStatus=(move.cat==='status'||move.kind==='status'||move.kind==='recover'||move.kind==='shield')&&move.power<=0;
 if(isStatus){
  if(b.paused||m.hp<=0||cooldowns[i]>now||b.energy<(move.cost||0))return false;
  const def=defFor(move);
  if(!def){note(this,'player','SEM EFEITO','#ffccaa');log(move.name+': efeito ainda não definido');return false;}
  const mode=def[0],self=this.v25?.stats?.player,enemy=this.v25?.stats?.enemy;
  if(!self||!enemy)return false;
  const types=[D[foe().id].type,D[foe().id].type2];
  if(mode==='condition'){
   const cond=def[1];
   const immune=(cond==='paralysis'&&types.includes('ground'))||
    (cond==='poison'&&types.some(t=>t==='poison'||t==='steel'))||
    (cond==='sleep'&&/powder|spore/i.test(move.name)&&types.includes('grass'));
   if(immune){note(this,'enemy','NÃO AFETA','#f8d6a4');log(move.name+': não afeta '+D[foe().id].name);return false;}
  }
  if(mode==='heal'&&m.hp>=m.maxHp){note(this,'player','PV CHEIO','#baf7dd');return false;}
  b.energy-=(move.cost||0);cooldowns[i]=now+safe(Number(move.cd)||5700,650,16000);
  this.attackActs.player=this.time+.36;
  let labels=[],targetSide='player';
  const stat=(target,k,change,side)=>{if(!SKILL_STAT[k])return;const prev=Number(target[k]??1);const next=safe(prev+change,.45,1.9);target[k]=next;
    const diff=next-prev;if(Math.abs(diff)>.0001)labels.push([side,SKILL_STAT[k]+' '+(diff>0?'+':'−')+Math.abs(diff).toFixed(2)+' x'+next.toFixed(2),diff>0?'#b8ffcc':'#ffc59f']);};
  switch(mode){
   case 'heal':{const hp=Math.min(m.maxHp-m.hp,Math.max(1,Math.round(m.maxHp*def[1])));m.hp=Math.min(m.maxHp,m.hp+hp);labels.push(['player','+'+hp+' PV','#b6ffd0']);break;}
   case 'buff':stat(self,def[1],def[2],'player');break;
   case 'debuff':stat(enemy,def[1],-def[2],'enemy');targetSide='enemy';break;
   case 'double':{const delta=def[3];const friendly=delta>=0;stat(friendly?self:enemy,def[1],delta,friendly?'player':'enemy');stat(friendly?self:enemy,def[2],delta*(key(move.name)==='calmmind'?.5:1),friendly?'player':'enemy');targetSide=friendly?'player':'enemy';break;}
   case 'condition':{targetSide='enemy';if(!enemy.conditions.includes(def[1]))enemy.conditions.push(def[1]);this.v342CondTimers||={};this.v342CondTimers[def[1]]=now+def[2];labels.push(['enemy',def[1].toUpperCase(),'#f5caff']);if(def[1]==='sleep')this.enemyAttackCD=Math.max(this.enemyAttackCD,2.4);break;}
   case 'shield':this.player.guard=Math.max(this.player.guard,def[1]);this.player.inv=Math.max(this.player.inv,.48);labels.push(['player','PROTEGIDO','#b4eeff']);break;
   case 'dodge':this.player.inv=Math.max(this.player.inv,1.1);labels.push(['player','ESQUIVA +','#b5eaff']);break;
   case 'cleanse':self.conditions=[];this.player.guard=Math.max(this.player.guard,1.2);labels.push(['player','STATUS REMOVIDO','#a7f9bd']);break;
   case 'reset':for(const stats of [self,enemy])for(const k of Object.keys(SKILL_STAT))stats[k]=1;labels.push(['player','ATRIBUTOS x1.00','#f4e5ff']);break;
   case 'weather':b.v32Weather=def[1];labels.push(['player',def[1].toUpperCase(),'#f6e7b0']);break;
  }
  for(const [side,tint,textColor] of labels.slice(0,3))note(this,side,tint,textColor);
  const at=targetSide==='enemy'?this.enemy:this.player;
  this.v351StatusCast={at:now,side:targetSide,type:move.type};
  this.particlesAt(at.x,at.y-this.h*.11,TYPE_COLOR[move.type]||'#a2f7d2',8,45);
  log(D[m.id].name+' usou '+move.name+'! '+(labels.map(x=>x[1]).join(' / ')||'efeito ativado'));
  this.v351StatusCount=(this.v351StatusCount||0)+1;
  persist();return true;
 }
 const e=b.energy;
 this.v351MovePreview=move;
 const result=oldMove.call(this,i);
 if(b===battle&&b.active&&((cooldowns[i]||0)>now && (cooldowns[i]||0)!==prior||b.energy<e)){
   this.v351CastCount=(this.v351CastCount||0)+1;
   if(move.type!=='electric'){
    this.v351Effects||=[];this.v351Effects.push({kind:'travel',move:move.name,type:move.type,at:now,end:now+670,from:[this.player.x,this.player.y-this.h*.13],to:[this.enemy.x,this.enemy.y-this.h*.12]});
    this.v351Effects=this.v351Effects.slice(-14);
   }
 }
 return result;
};
// Impacto próprio, sincronizado ao dano e independente do sprite estático.
const prevHit=BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget;
BattleEngine.prototype.hitTarget=function(side,type,power){
 const result=prevHit.call(this,side,type,power);
 if(battle?.active&&type!=='electric'){
  const victim=side==='enemy'?this.enemy:this.player;
  this.v351Effects||=[];
  this.v351Effects.push({kind:'impact',type,move:this.v351MovePreview?.name||'',at:performance.now(),end:performance.now()+420,
   from:[victim.x,victim.y-this.h*.1],to:[victim.x,victim.y-this.h*.1]});
  this.v351Effects=this.v351Effects.slice(-18);
 }
 return result;
};
// Renderizar trajetórias desenhadas de verdade, não PNGs colados sobre a arena.
function path(ctx,pts,color,width,a=1){if(pts.length<2)return;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=a;ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.lineWidth=width;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(...pts[0]);for(const p of pts.slice(1))ctx.lineTo(...p);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
function tri(ctx,x,y,angle,size,color,alpha){ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(angle);ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,-size*1.3);ctx.lineTo(-size*.64,size*.7);ctx.lineTo(size*.64,size*.7);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}
function dot(ctx,x,y,r,color,alpha){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}
function orbit(ctx,x,y,t,type,h){
 const total=type==='paralysis'?4:5,scale=h*.058;
 for(let i=0;i<total;i++){
  const a=t*(type==='paralysis'?.009:.004)+i*Math.PI*2/total,px=x+Math.cos(a)*scale,py=y+Math.sin(a)*scale*.65;
  if(type==='paralysis')path(ctx,[[px-4,py-4],[px+1,py-7],[px-1,py],[px+5,py-3]],'#ffe776',2,.92);
  else if(type==='freeze'||type==='frozen'){
   path(ctx,[[px-4,py],[px+4,py],[px,py-4],[px,py+4]],'#b5f1ff',2,.85);
  }else if(type==='burn'){tri(ctx,px,py,a,5,'#ff9e45',.87);tri(ctx,px,py+2,a,2.5,'#fff1a5',.8);}
  else if(type==='poison'){dot(ctx,px,py,3.5,'#ce83ff',.72);dot(ctx,px-1,py-1,1.3,'#f5d3ff',.9);}
  else if(type==='confusion'){dot(ctx,px,py,3.5,i%2?'#ffc6ec':'#e5d65a',.8);}
  else if(type==='sleep'){ctx.save();ctx.font='bold 10px system-ui';ctx.fillStyle='#d8f5ff';ctx.globalAlpha=.85;ctx.fillText('Z',px,py-9);ctx.restore();}
  else if(type==='seed'){tri(ctx,px,py,a,4,'#8adb65',.75);}
 }
}
const COLORS={fire:['#ff
